---
name: gitnexus-pr-review
description: "Use when the user wants to review a pull request, understand what a PR changes, assess risk of merging, or check for missing test coverage. Examples: \"Review this PR\", \"What does PR #42 change?\", \"Is this PR safe to merge?\""

# PR Review with GitNexus

## When to Use

- "Review this PR"
- "What does PR #42 change?"
- "Is this safe to merge?"
- "What's the blast radius of this PR?"
- "Are there missing tests for this PR?"
- Reviewing someone else's code changes before merge

## Workflow

```
1. gh pr diff <number>                                    → Get the raw diff
2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})  → Map diff to affected flows
3. For each changed symbol:
   gitnexus_impact({target: "<symbol>", direction: "upstream"})    → Blast radius per change
4. gitnexus_context({name: "<key symbol>"})               → Understand callers/callees
5. READ gitnexus://repo/{name}/processes                   → Check affected execution flows
6. Summarize findings with risk assessment
```

> If "Index is stale" → run `npx gitnexus analyze` in terminal before reviewing.

## Checklist

```
- [ ] Fetch PR diff (gh pr diff or git diff base...head)
- [ ] gitnexus_detect_changes to map changes to affected execution flows
- [ ] gitnexus_impact on each non-trivial changed symbol
- [ ] Review d=1 items (WILL BREAK) — are callers updated?
- [ ] gitnexus_context on key changed symbols to understand full picture
- [ ] Check if affected processes have test coverage
- [ ] Assess overall risk level
- [ ] Write review summary with findings
```

## Review Dimensions

| Dimension | How GitNexus Helps |
| --- | --- |
| **Correctness** | `context` shows callers — are they all compatible with the change? |
| **Blast radius** | `impact` shows d=1/d=2/d=3 dependents — anything missed? |
| **Completeness** | `detect_changes` shows all affected flows — are they all handled? |
| **Test coverage** | `impact({includeTests: true})` shows which tests touch changed code |
| **Breaking changes** | d=1 upstream items that aren't updated in the PR = potential breakage |

## Risk Assessment

| Signal | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Changes touch <3 symbols, 0-1 processes | LOW |
| Changes touch 3-10 symbols, 2-5 processes | MEDIUM |
| Changes touch >10 symbols or many processes | HIGH |
| Changes touch auth, payments, or data integrity code | CRITICAL |
| d=1 callers exist outside the PR diff | Potential breakage — flag it |

## Tools

**gitnexus_detect_changes** — map PR diff to affected execution flows:

```
gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})

→ Changed: 8 symbols in 4 files
→ Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow, WebhookHandler
→ Risk: MEDIUM
```

**gitnexus_impact** — blast radius per changed symbol:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})

→ d=1 (WILL BREAK):
  - processCheckout (src/checkout.ts:42) [CALLS, 100%]
  - webhookHandler (src/webhooks.ts:15) [CALLS, 100%]

→ d=2 (LIKELY AFFECTED):
  - checkoutRouter (src/routes/checkout.ts:22) [CALLS, 95%]
```

**gitnexus_impact with tests** — check test coverage:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream", includeTests: true})

→ Tests that cover this symbol:
  - validatePayment.test.ts [direct]
  - checkout.integration.test.ts [via processCheckout]
```

**gitnexus_context** — understand a changed symbol's role:

```
gitnexus_context({name: "validatePayment"})

→ Incoming calls: processCheckout, webhookHandler
→ Outgoing calls: verifyCard, fetchRates
→ Processes: CheckoutFlow (step 3/7), RefundFlow (step 1/5)
```

## Example: "Review PR #42"

```
1. gh pr diff 42 > /tmp/pr42.diff
   → 4 files changed: payments.ts, checkout.ts, types.ts, utils.ts

2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})
   → Changed symbols: validatePayment, PaymentInput, formatAmount
   → Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow
   → Risk: MEDIUM

3. gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: processCheckout, webhookHandler (WILL BREAK)
   → webhookHandler is NOT in the PR diff — potential breakage!

4. gitnexus_impact({target: "PaymentInput", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: validatePayment (in PR), createPayment (NOT in PR)
   → createPayment uses the old PaymentInput shape — breaking change!

5. gitnexus_context({name: "formatAmount"})
   → Called by 12 functions — but change is backwards-compatible (added optional param)

6. Review summary:
   - MEDIUM risk — 3 changed symbols affect 2 execution flows
   - BUG: webhookHandler calls validatePayment but isn't updated for new signature
   - BUG: createPayment depends on PaymentInput type which changed
   - OK: formatAmount change is backwards-compatible
   - Tests: checkout.test.ts covers processCheckout path, but no webhook test
```

## Review Output Format

Structure your review as:

```markdown
## PR Review: <title>

**Risk: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL**

### Changes Summary
- <N> symbols changed across <M> files
- <P> execution flows affected

### Findings
1. **[severity]** Description of finding
   - Evidence from GitNexus tools
   - Affected callers/flows

### Missing Coverage
- Callers not updated in PR: ...
- Untested flows: ...

### Recommendation
APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES / NEEDS DISCUSSION
```

 # Plexco — Setup dev · staging · production
 
 > **Version:** v2.1 · 2026-07-16
 > **Authority:** DD-04 = DigitalOcean co-location: App Platform + Managed PostgreSQL HA in Singapore,
 > connected through the `sgp1` VPC. The accepted record is
 > [`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`](../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md).
 > **Current repository state:** the local Docker Compose definition exists and validates; Docker-daemon
 > availability remains machine state and **must be checked before each run**. The daemon was started for the
 > 2026-07-16 closure and the canonical PostgreSQL/RLS/auth suite passed; the test container was then stopped.
 > The production DigitalOcean App Spec and deployment workflows are still an infra-slice deliverable.
 > `infra/fly.toml` is a bounded fallback artifact, not the production runbook.
 
 Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
 and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.
 
 ---
 
 ## 0. Target environment model
 
 ```text
 local development
   docker compose + local PostgreSQL
             │
             │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
             ▼
 ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
   production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
   sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
             │
             │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
             ▼
 production on DigitalOcean
   2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
 ```
 
 | Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
 | **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
 | **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
 | **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
 
 Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
 production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
 automatically after the TTL.
 
 ---
 
 ## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
 
 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
-DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. Record
-written evidence for every production dependency before creating the App Spec:
+DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
+table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
+and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
+`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
+contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
 
-| Dependency | Required answer |
-|---|---|
-| SePay | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Inbound webhook verification alone does not imply that it does. |
-| ApiPay | Does enterprise provisioning restrict API callers by fixed source IP? |
-| Zalo Bot | Does the send API require fixed outbound IP? |
-| Meta Messenger | Does the Graph API require fixed outbound IP? |
-
-- All answers **no** → continue with App Platform + VPC.
-- Any answer **yes** → stop. Do not disable the private DB path silently. Activate the bounded Fly+DO
-  fallback or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
+| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
+| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
+| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
+
+**Gate status: `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`** — SePay, ApiPay and Zalo Bot are `UNRESOLVED` (2026-07-18). No
+overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
+affirmatively addresses outbound caller source-IP policy, and inference from omitted registration steps does
+not meet this gate's evidence bar. The owner must obtain an attributable support or contractual response from
+each of the three providers (for ApiPay: the DD-30 enterprise onboarding contract explicitly, not only the
+BYO API) before this gate can close.
+
+- All four rows attributable and **NO** → record `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` plus the closure marker.
+  That permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review only**; it does
+  not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning.
+- Any attributable **YES** → record `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED`. That blocks the DO App Platform +
+  VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS
+  substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+- Until the gate closes, do not create the App Spec and do not disable the private DB path silently.
 
 ### 1.2 Repository preconditions
 
 Before the infra slice runs:
 
 ```bash
 test -f infra/Dockerfile
 test -f infra/entrypoint.sh
 test -f .github/workflows/ci.yml
 test -f infra/app.prod.yaml       # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 test -f infra/app.staging.yaml    # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 ```
 
 If either App Spec is absent, this runbook is a deployment contract only: **do not improvise a console-only
 production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.
 
 ---
 
 ## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
 
 Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
 Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
 
 | Resource | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|
 | App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
 | App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
 | VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
 | PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
 | DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
 | Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
 | Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
 
 Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
 ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.
 
 ---
 
 ## 3. One image, three App Platform components
 
 The App Specs deploy the same immutable image as three components. Use a unique release tag such as the git SHA,
 resolve and record its registry digest, and prohibit mutable `latest` promotion.
 
 | Component | Day-1 production shape | Command | DB identity | Scaling contract |
 |---|---:|---|---|---|
 | `web` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh web` | `app` pooled URL + `app` direct listener URL | Request-based RPS/p95 autoscaling is eligible on shared CPU; CPU autoscaling requires dedicated CPU |
 | `discovery` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh discovery` | `readonly` pooled URL on the primary at Day 1 | Bounded pool + timeout + CDN + circuit breaker; read-only node only after telemetry trigger |
 | `worker` | `1 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb-fixed` | `infra/entrypoint.sh worker` | `app_worker` direct/session URL | One durable consumer at Day 1; add a second scalable worker only after idempotency and queue-lag gates pass |
 
 App Platform high availability for the HTTP paths requires at least two containers, hence `web=2` and
 `discovery=2`. The worker starts single by design; pg-boss durability is in PostgreSQL, not local disk.
 
 ### Required App Spec controls
 
 - VPC attachment to the environment's `sgp1` VPC; no dedicated egress-IP block on the VPC path.
 - Set `TRUSTED_INGRESS_PROVIDER=digitalocean` on every public HTTP component. Application rate limits trust
   only DigitalOcean's overwritten `do-connecting-ip`; they never fall back to `X-Forwarded-For` or a header
   owned by another provider. The bounded Fly fallback must instead set `fly`; `direct` is local-only.
 - Health checks on `/healthz` for both HTTP services.
 - Separate component-level environment variables and secrets.
 - Explicit instance size/count; no console-only drift.
 - Image release tag/digest recorded in deployment evidence.
 - Deployment-failure and domain-failure alerts.
 - TTL metadata and destruction job for staging.
 
 ---
 
 ## 4. Database roles and connection variables
 
 Create four roles per database. The migration/owner credential is never injected into an App Platform runtime
 component.
 
 | Role | Runtime surface | Permissions | Connection mode |
 |---|---|---|---|
 | `migration` | CI deployment job only | DDL, migrations, grants, controlled queue provisioning | Direct; job-scoped secret |
 | `app` | `web` | Tenant RLS, application functions; `NOSUPERUSER NOBYPASSRLS` | Transaction pool for requests; separate direct connection for `LISTEN` |
 | `readonly` | `discovery` | `SELECT` only on `public_sessions` and approved public views; zero grant on canonical money/PII tables | Bounded transaction pool |
 | `app_worker` | `worker` | Narrow worker functions, pg-boss/outbox/projector operations | Direct/session connection |
 
 The application contract intentionally stays provider-neutral:
@@ -197,154 +211,155 @@ pull request
 App creation/update is automated from reviewed specs:
 
 ```bash
 # First create; use `doctl apps update` for later reconciliations.
 doctl apps create --spec infra/app.staging.yaml
 doctl apps create --spec infra/app.prod.yaml
 ```
 
 The CI workflow supplies the immutable release value to both specs. Do not rebuild for production and do not
 promote a mutable tag.
 
 ### Required CI order
 
 1. Validate target PostgreSQL version/extensions and roles.
 2. Run migrations with `DATABASE_URL_MIGRATION`.
 3. Run `infra/entrypoint.sh provision` using the same job-scoped credential.
 4. Deploy/reconcile components.
 5. Wait for health/readiness; execute smoke and reconnect checks.
 
 Migration and queue provisioning must not be implemented as an App Platform runtime command that injects the DDL
 credential into all components.
 
 ---
 
 ## 7. URLs and channel configuration
 
 Use distinct domains and provider registrations:
 
 | Surface | Local/integration | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|---|
 | Google OAuth redirect | local/tunnel client | staging client + staging domain | production client + production domain |
 | Meta Login/Messenger | developer/test app or test users | staging app/page | live reviewed app/page |
 | Zalo | test club/bot | staging club/bot | per-club production tokens encrypted in DB |
 | SePay/ApiPay | sandbox/test account | staging account | production accounts/webhooks |
 | PWA/API base | localhost/tunnel | staging domain | production domain |
 
 Webhook updates are idempotent deployment steps. Verify the registered callback after every domain/app change; do
 not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
 
 ---
 
 ## 8. Rollback and recovery
 
 ### Application rollback
 
 - Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
 - Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
 - If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.
 
 ### Database recovery
 
 - Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
 - Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
   ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
 - Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
   migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.
 
 ---
 
 ## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
 
 Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
 
 1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
 2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
 3. runs the selected gates;
 4. exports drill evidence;
 5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
 6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
 
 Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
 silently become an always-on production mirror.
 
 ---
 
 ## 10. Production acceptance checklist
 
 This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
 slice:
 
-- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence.
+- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — still open: §1.1 gate status
+      `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE` (SePay/ApiPay/Zalo Bot unresolved, checked 2026-07-18).
 - [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
 - [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
 - [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.
 - [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` pass rolling-deploy and health tests.
 - [ ] Discovery pool cap, `statement_timeout`, reserved money connection budget, CDN/rate limits and circuit breaker pass load tests.
 - [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.
 - [ ] SSE re-subscribes and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
 - [ ] PITR and off-provider restores meet measured RPO/RTO and reconciliation gates.
 - [ ] KMS rotation and decrypt-failure paths pass.
 - [ ] Ephemeral staging reproduces from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
 - [ ] Load evidence captures RPS/concurrency, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate, egress and projected cost.
 - [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
 - [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.
 
 ---
 
 ## 11. Cost planning
 
 | Environment | Planning envelope |
 |---|---:|
 | Local/integration smoke | `$0–15/month`, depending on whether a shared public callback target is kept |
 | Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |
 | Production Day 1 | **`$120–135/month`** |
 | Production + expected staging | **`$128–153/month`** |
 
 The production baseline includes two `web`, two `discovery`, one worker, DO PostgreSQL primary+standby,
 Cloudflare Free, Sentry Developer, KMS, low-volume logs/offsite backup and egress allowances. Physical read-only
 node, second worker, WAF Pro, Sentry Team and resizing are telemetry-triggered upgrades, not Day-1 requirements.
 
 Host count does not select a resource tier. Scale purchases require staging/production measurements and the
 triggers in DD-04 §5.2.
 
 ---
 
 ## 12. Bounded Fly fallback
 
 `infra/fly.toml` is retained only because DD-04 pre-authorizes fallback `(a)` when a production dependency proves
 that fixed outbound IP is mandatory and App Platform+VPC cannot satisfy it.
 
 Activating fallback requires a recorded gate result and a separate reviewed App/DB network plan:
 
 - compute on Fly with static egress;
 - DO PostgreSQL reached over public TLS with `sslmode=verify-full` and IP allowlist;
 - the same four DB roles, immutable image, migration-job boundary, backup/restore and acceptance gates;
 - a new cost reconciliation before procurement.
 
 Do not use the old “three Fly apps + Fly Postgres” topology. If fallback is not activated, no production Fly
 resource is provisioned.
 
 ---
 
 ## 13. Anti-patterns
 
 - One app/database or shared secrets across staging and production.
 - Console-only production resources that IaC cannot reproduce.
 - Migration/owner credential in any runtime component.
 - Mutable image tags or rebuilding between staging and production.
 - Treating the HA standby as the discovery replica.
 - Buying a read-only node from host count rather than measured contention.
 - Disabling VPC to obtain a fixed egress IP without activating the approved fallback/design branch.
 - Treating provider PITR as the off-provider backup.
 - Leaving ephemeral staging alive without an owner and TTL.
 
 ---

# DD-04 Final Decision — Co-locate compute + PostgreSQL on DigitalOcean

> **Status:** ✅ Accepted<br>
> **Decision date:** 2026-07-16<br>
> **Revision:** 2 — cost-optimized, upgradeable, provider-portable baseline<br>
> **Owner:** Founder<br>
> **Scope:** production compute, PostgreSQL hosting, network posture, operating cost, scaling, backup,
> and provider-exit contract<br>
> **Supersedes:** recommendation `(a) giữ Fly + public TLS` in
> [`dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md`](dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md). Provider facts and hard gates in
> that brief remain useful; this record is the final choice.

## 1. Decision

Choose **QĐ-1 (c): co-locate compute and database on DigitalOcean in Singapore**:

- **Compute:** DigitalOcean App Platform, one immutable OCI image deployed as three component types:
  `web`, `discovery`, and `worker`.
- **Database:** DigitalOcean Managed PostgreSQL, **HA cluster with at least one standby node**.
- **Network:** App Platform and PostgreSQL connect through the same DigitalOcean VPC in `sgp`/`sgp1`;
  PostgreSQL is restricted to trusted sources on that private network.
- **Database security remains defence-in-depth:** TLS hostname/CA verification, four least-privilege DB
  roles, separate pooled and direct/session connection strings, and no migration credential in runtime
  components.
- **Region:** Singapore for both compute and database.
- **Day-1 planning budget:** **`$120–135/month`**, including HA/PITR, private DB connectivity, two
  `web`, two `discovery`, one durable worker, KMS, low-volume observability, and encrypted offsite backup.

This is the highest-benefit option for Plexco because it combines a private DB path, managed failover,
predictable operations, and one production-provider boundary without buying capacity before telemetry
requires it. The lower Day-1 cost comes from trigger-based upgrades, **not** from removing HA, PITR,
RLS, encryption, backup, or fail-closed money controls.

This decision changes the deployment target currently represented by [`infra/fly.toml`](../infra/fly.toml).
It does **not** reverse DD-10: DD-10 chooses Next.js App Router, standalone containers, and no Vercel; it
does not canonically require Fly.io.

## 2. Non-negotiable requirements

1. Managed PostgreSQL with automatic failover, PITR, and contractual uptime SLA.
2. Singapore region for the steady-state production topology.
3. Private VPC DB path; public TLS + static-IP allowlist is a fallback posture, not equivalent to private.
4. Transaction-pooled URL for request traffic plus direct/session URLs for SSE `LISTEN/NOTIFY` and
   pg-boss workers.
5. Four connection-level roles with least privilege: runtime web, discovery/readonly, worker, and
   migration.
6. RLS, allocation, idempotency, and money paths remain fail-closed.
7. KMS envelope encryption, encrypted offsite logical backup, and tested restore outside DigitalOcean.
8. PostgreSQL portability: no provider-exclusive data model, queue, or business logic.
9. Failover, reconnect, rolling-deploy, restore, and provider-exit drills must pass before production
   acceptance.

## 3. Cost model and assumptions

All figures are **USD/month, list-price planning estimates verified 2026-07-16**. They exclude VAT/tax,
paid support, third-party API fees, unusual egress, migration labour, and usage beyond the stated free or
low-volume allowances. Procurement must re-price before launch.

| Assumption | Value used |
|---|---|
| Billing month | 730 hours where hourly pricing applies |
| Production processes | `web` + `discovery` + `worker` from the same immutable image |
| Day-1 compute | Two 1 GiB scalable `web`, two 1 GiB scalable `discovery`, one fixed 1 GiB `worker` |
| DO database | 2 GiB/1 vCPU primary `$30.45` + matching standby `$30.45` = `$60.90` |
| Edge | Cloudflare Free at Day 1; Pro only on a capability/abuse trigger |
| Error monitoring | Sentry Developer at Day 1; Team only on a team/integration/quota trigger |
| Secrets | One AWS KMS key plus low request volume: `$1–3` planning allowance |
| Logs and offsite backup | Low-volume logs plus encrypted logical exports to R2: `$0–5` |
| Traffic/variance buffer | `$0–8`; raise only from measured egress or retained-log volume |
| Staging | Full production-shape environment created from IaC for 48–96 hours per release/drill, then destroyed |

Tenant/host count is **not** a capacity formula. The prior 100/1000/2000-host envelopes are superseded
because they multiplied unbenchmarked infrastructure assumptions. Size from active concurrency, webhook
RPS, query time, DB CPU/RAM/connections, queue lag, egress, and p95/p99 latency.

## 4. Option comparison after cost optimization

These ranges compare a small-production shape with HA where the option can meet that gate. Paid WAF,
paid Sentry, and a permanent read replica are excluded unless their trigger fires.

| Option | Estimated monthly cost | Security / operations | Decision |
|---|---:|---|---|
| **DO App Platform + DO Managed PG HA** | **`$120–135`** | Private VPC, one provider, managed compute + DB | **Selected** |
| DO Droplets + DO Managed PG HA | `~$100–115` | Private VPC, but OS, rollout, patching, capacity and LB ownership move to the team | Upgrade/fallback substrate, not Day-1 default |
| Fly + DO Managed PG HA | `~$105–120` | Public DB path with `verify-full` + static-IP allowlist; two providers | Bounded fallback if §7.1 fails |
| Fly + Neon Scale | `~$90–105` at 0.25 CU; `~$130–145` at 0.5 CU | Public DB path, two providers, variable CU bill, always-on tuning | Not chosen |
| AWS Fargate + Neon PrivateLink | `~$225–350` | Strict-private, but ECS/ALB/NAT/endpoints/CloudWatch add cost and operating surface | Not chosen |
| DO App Platform + single-node DO PG | `~$90–100` | Removes standby HA and the 99.95% DB SLA gate | Rejected |
| Supabase / Railway / self-hosted PG | Plan- or quote-dependent | Fails the combined Day-1 HA/SLA/private-path/low-ops gates, or costs more | Rejected for production baseline |

The selected option is not the absolute cheapest line item. It maximizes total project benefit: about
`$5–30/month` above the credible low-cost alternatives buys a private DB path, one incident boundary,
and lower solo-team operating load. The AWS/private topology buys more infrastructure than the current
single-region product needs.

## 5. Selected-option cost detail

### 5.1 Day-1 production baseline

| Item | Calculation | Monthly estimate |
|---|---:|---:|
| `web` | `2 × $12` scalable 1 GiB | `$24.00` |
| `discovery` | `2 × $12` scalable 1 GiB | `$24.00` |
| `worker` | `1 × $10` fixed 1 GiB | `$10.00` |
| DO Managed PostgreSQL HA | primary `$30.45` + standby `$30.45` | `$60.90` |
| Cloudflare | Free WAF subset, one rate-limit rule, unmetered DDoS protection | `$0` |
| Sentry | Developer: one user, error monitoring, tracing, email alerts | `$0` |
| AWS KMS | one key + low request volume; cache data keys for envelope encryption | `$1–3` |
| Logs + encrypted R2 offsite backup | low-volume allowance; money/identity audit remains DB-canonical | `$0–5` |
| Egress/variance buffer | measured, not pre-purchased | `$0–8` |
| **Planning total** | exact subtotal `$118.90` before variable allowances | **`$120–135`** |

The fixed `$10` worker is intentional at Day 1: there is one durable consumer until idempotency and
duplicate-delivery tests prove horizontal safety. When a second worker is justified, move to two scalable
1 GiB workers; the incremental planning cost is approximately `$14/month`.

### 5.2 Trigger-based upgrades

| Upgrade | Trigger — require telemetry or a failed gate | Approximate monthly delta |
|---|---|---:|
| Second scalable worker | Sustained queue lag breaches SLO after safe concurrency tuning; idempotency and duplicate-delivery tests green | `+$14` from the fixed-worker baseline |
| DO read-only node | Discovery load causes measured primary contention or money-path p95/p99 regression after pool/rate/cache controls | `+$30.45` minimum at the current 2 GiB primary size |
| Cloudflare Pro | Free managed rules + one rate-limit rule cannot express the required control, or observed abuse requires it | `+$20` annual equivalent / `+$25` month-to-month |
| Sentry Team | More than one operator, third-party integrations, or Developer quota/retention is insufficient | `+$26` annual equivalent |
| Paid log retention | Free/low-volume retention cannot meet incident, audit, or compliance needs | Usage/policy-based |
| App component 1→2 GiB | Component CPU/RAM or latency remains saturated after horizontal tuning | `+$13` per upgraded instance |
| PostgreSQL 2→4 GiB HA | DB CPU/RAM/connections/query latency breach the capacity gate under representative load | `+$60.90` for primary + standby together |

Do **not** use the HA standby as the default discovery replica. DigitalOcean allows reads from standby,
but warns that read load can overwhelm its ability to replace the primary. A DO read-only node is also
not a cheap same-VPC copy: it must be at least the primary size, is geographically separate, and primary
replication traffic is SSL-encrypted over the public network. Add it only when benchmark evidence pays for
that tradeoff.

### 5.3 Read isolation without a Day-1 replica

`discovery` remains isolated from money workloads through all of the following:

- a projection-only DB role with zero grants on canonical money/PII tables;
- a separate bounded pool with a hard connection cap and statement timeout;
- CDN caching for public projections, edge and application rate limits, and cache-safe invalidation;
- a circuit breaker that degrades discovery to stale data, `429`, or `503` before web/worker pools suffer;
- reserved connection budgets for `web`, `worker`, and session-mode listeners;
- a physical read-only node only after load tests show the controls above are insufficient.

### 5.4 Ephemeral staging instead of an always-on mirror

Provision a full production-shape staging environment from IaC for 48–96 hours around each release,
load/soak test, failover drill, restore drill, or provider-exit rehearsal, then destroy it automatically
with a TTL. At the Day-1 shape this averages approximately **`$8–18/month`**, making the expected
production-plus-staging envelope **`$128–153/month`**. Keep a minimal non-production smoke surface only
if a persistent integration callback requires it; it is not a second production stack.

## 6. Why option (c) maximizes project benefit

1. **Security:** the steady-state DB path is private; public exposure is not accepted silently to save cost.
2. **Money correctness:** managed standby failover, 99.95% DB SLA, PITR, isolated connection pools, and
   one-provider incident handling reduce failure modes around writes and reconnects.
3. **Maintainability:** one account, region, VPC, bill, and managed compute layer are a better solo-team
   trade than maintaining VMs or AWS network fabric for a small saving.
4. **Scalability:** App Platform can scale components horizontally, and DO Managed PostgreSQL can resize
   CPU/RAM/storage without downtime; expensive capacity is added from telemetry.
5. **Architecture fit:** Plexco needs durable pg-boss and `LISTEN/NOTIFY` session connections, so there is
   no dependency on a serverless DB scale-to-zero lifecycle.
6. **Portability:** immutable images, standard PostgreSQL, configuration-only connection endpoints, IaC,
   and an executable migration contract prevent provider choice from leaking into business logic.

## 7. Risks and acceptance gates

### 7.1 Hard gate: VPC versus dedicated egress IP

DigitalOcean App Platform cannot enable VPC network access and dedicated egress IPs at the same time.
Before implementation, verify whether Zalo, Messenger, SePay, ApiPay, or any other production dependency
requires a fixed outbound source IP.

- If none requires it, proceed with option (c).
- If one requires it, **do not silently disable the private DB path**. Use option `(a) Fly + DO` as the
  bounded fallback, or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with both private DB access and controlled egress.

| Dependency | Question to answer | Verification source |
|---|---|---|
| **SePay** | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Does inbound webhook verification require a fixed Plexco IP? | SePay partner docs/support; connector spike CO-OQ-2/3/7 |
| **ApiPay** | Does enterprise auto-provisioning (DD-30) restrict API calls by source IP? | ApiPay enterprise onboarding |
| **Zalo Bot** | Does the send API require fixed egress IP? | Zalo Bot API docs; DD-14/15 spike |
| **Messenger (Meta)** | Does the Graph API require fixed egress IP? | Meta docs |
| **Conclusion** | Any confirmed “yes” breaks option (c) as-is and activates the bounded fallback/design branch | Recorded provider evidence |

### 7.2 Provider-neutral application contract

Co-location intentionally accepts DigitalOcean account/region concentration instead of buying an AWS
control plane on Day 1. That risk is acceptable only while the off-provider backup, restore rehearsal,
immutable image, and migration contract below remain green.

The application must satisfy all of these before production:

- build one immutable OCI image that runs on App Platform, Fly, Droplets, DOKS, ECS, or equivalent;
- read provider endpoints only from `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_URL_DIRECT`, and
  `DATABASE_URL_LISTEN`; no provider hostname or SDK in domain/business code;
- use standard PostgreSQL plus an extension allowlist verified against at least two credible target
  providers; migrations own schema, roles, grants, RLS, extensions, and sequence setup;
- keep queue durability in PostgreSQL/pg-boss and realtime durability in canonical tables/outbox;
  `LISTEN/NOTIFY` is a wake-up hint, not the durable record;
- keep provider-specific deployment modules behind IaC/config boundaries;
- do not add a permanent cross-cloud DB proxy only for portability: it adds latency and a new single point
  of failure without removing migration work.

### 7.3 Upgrade paths that preserve application performance

- **App Platform:** scale `web` and `discovery` horizontally. Request-rate and p95-response-time
  autoscaling can run on shared or dedicated compute; CPU-utilization autoscaling requires dedicated CPU.
  Start shared/scalable and move to dedicated only when CPU isolation or CPU-based policy is justified.
- **PostgreSQL:** resize CPU/RAM/storage and enable storage autoscaling before introducing a new data layer.
  DO documents these resize operations as no-downtime, but Plexco still validates reconnects and p95/p99.
- **Compute substrate:** if App Platform cost or network features become limiting, blue-green the same OCI
  image to DO Droplets or DOKS in the same VPC, then drain old instances. The DB path and app contracts stay
  unchanged.
- **Database provider:** provision the target at equal-or-greater tested capacity and the same or newer
  PostgreSQL major version. Migrate with logical replication and a bounded write freeze; do not restore a
  stale dump over a live target.

### 7.4 Cross-provider migration and rollback contract

Required migration flow:

```text
source live
  → validate target versions/extensions/roles
  → initial copy
  → logical replication
  → shadow reads + checksums + ledger/outbox reconciliation
  → freeze writes
  → drain pg-boss worker and all mutation traffic
  → replication lag = 0
  → sync sequences
  → switch DATABASE_URL* atomically
  → restart/re-subscribe LISTEN/NOTIFY consumers
  → resume worker and writes
  → observe before decommissioning source
```

PostgreSQL logical replication does **not** replicate DDL/schema changes, sequence state, large objects,
views, or roles/grants. Apply and verify those separately. During shadow validation the target is read-only;
dual-writing money data is prohibited because it creates split-brain and makes rollback ambiguous.

Cutover acceptance gates under the same production-shaped load:

- zero lost or duplicated charge, transaction, allocation, outbox, or audit records;
- public reads remain available; mutation pause is **≤5 minutes** in the rehearsal at Day-1 data size;
- target error rate is no worse by more than `0.1` percentage point and p95/p99 latency is no worse by
  more than `10%` versus the source benchmark;
- target connection usage remains below `80%` of its safe connection budget and queue lag returns to SLO;
- SSE listeners re-subscribe and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations;
- DNS/connection TTL, credential rotation, and old-source isolation are proven in the runbook.

Rollback is asymmetric:

- **Before** the target accepts writes: switch connections back to the still-current source.
- **After** the target accepts writes: never point back to a stale source. Use forward-fix, or prove reverse
  replication and sequence/ledger reconciliation before rollback.
- Keep the source read-only for at least **72 hours and one completed money-reconciliation cycle**, then

docs/standards/decisions.md-99-- **Quyết định (owner):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-100-  - **DB lưu suffix trần UPPERCASE, 4 ký tự**, alphabet **A-Z + 2-9 trừ `0 O 1 I L`** (31, không phải Crockford); unique per-tenant; **immutable**; `CHECK (format + ≥1 chữ cái, loại toàn số)`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-101-  - **Prefix = bảng global `pay_prefix_registry(prefix PK, tenant_id, state current|retired, locked_at)` — nguồn DUY NHẤT, KHÔNG cột `tenants.pay_prefix`.** `UNIQUE one-current-per-tenant`; retired KHÔNG tái dụng (PK cho 1 tx thắng). **MVP: prefix IMMUTABLE vĩnh viễn sau code đầu tiên** (bỏ controlled-change — `memberships` chỉ lưu suffix nên đổi prefix sẽ resolve nhầm; tương lai cần thì phải có mapping `(membership_id,prefix,suffix,issued_at)`). **Choke-point atomic** phát-code ↔ đổi-prefix (`SELECT current-prefix FOR UPDATE` cùng lock order). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-102-  - **Recognition = resolver `own | foreign | none`** (narrow SECURITY DEFINER, fail-closed — role `app` không blanket cross-tenant, `0009`). **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`, không false-issued). Prefix BẮT BUỘC; regex thêm `-?`. **Memo-level precedence:** hard-token (foreign / own-prefix+unissued / dash-lạ / error / >1-candidate) → HARD unmatched (thắng name-fallback); ≥2 distinct own → unmatched; đúng 1 own → resolve; chỉ `none`(`PAYMENT`) → name-fallback.
docs/standards/decisions.md-103-  - **Allocation = INVARIANT** qua **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (pay_code chỉ resolve MEMBERSHIP; ledger quyết charge): auto `exact`/`over`/`under`(FIFO partial, edge #8) **+ unknown-fee-phía-sau (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; manual **unknown-fee-đầu**/`already-paid`; ≥2 code / provider-code-mismatch → unmatched.
docs/standards/decisions.md:104:  - **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở core. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30) — parse `data.content` (schema reverify khi build); **SePay = fallback**, `code` chỉ evidence; giữ webhook nhận tx không-code (không `skip_if_no_code`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-105-  - **Merge:** pay_code giữ trên tombstone; recognition **follow `merged_into_membership_id`** (tenant/status/cycle guards). `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện.
docs/standards/decisions.md:106:- **Propagation ĐÃ làm ở SPEC (2026-07-13):** `identity-model` §merge + `feature_identity_tech` (pay_code tombstone follow-through) ✅ · connector `feature_connector_onboarding.md` 4.3b + `_tech` M2 (ApiPay enterprise = Phase-1 **primary**) + **schema `provider_connections.auth_mode += 'enterprise'`** (CHECK shape đầy đủ per provider/auth_mode; platform credential từ KMS, không per-tenant) + **CO-OQ-6 đóng** (`skip_if_no_code=0`, adapter-only) ✅ · `feature_payment_reconcile_tech` **§4 worker pipeline** (`normalize→discover→resolve→precedence→providerCode-crosscheck→plan`) + `bank_transactions.provider_code_enc` + §8 fixtures ✅.
docs/standards/decisions.md-107-- **Propagation CÒN LẠI (khi build P4, code):** wire core + **thay tests** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`); implement **`discoverPayCodeCandidates` + resolver narrow fn + `planIncomingAllocations()`**; migration **`pay_prefix_registry`** (one-current-per-tenant, choke-point) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code`.

docs/standards/decisions.md-123-## DD-04 — Hosting PostgreSQL · ✅ Accepted (2026-07-16) — Option (c) DO co-locate
docs/standards/decisions.md-124-
docs/standards/decisions.md-125-> ✅ **FINAL (2026-07-16, owner):** chọn **QĐ-1 (c) — co-locate compute + Postgres trên DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG HA-standby, **cùng VPC private**, Singapore). Supersede đề xuất (a) trong brief. Đổi deployment target **Fly → DO** (KHÔNG đảo DD-10 — App Platform chạy standalone container). Lý do: private DB by construction + managed HA/SLA/PITR + một provider (đơn giản cho solo dev) + fit `LISTEN/NOTIFY` session-pool (không scale-to-zero) + portability. Record đầy đủ: `../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-126->
docs/standards/decisions.md:127:> ⚠️ **Pre-build gate (§7.1) — CHƯA verify:** DO App Platform **không bật đồng thời** VPC-private-DB **và** dedicated egress-IP. **Phải xác minh** SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger có yêu cầu fixed outbound source-IP không **TRƯỚC khi dựng DO App Spec**; nếu có → dùng fallback **(a) Fly + DO**, **KHÔNG** âm thầm tắt private-DB path. Mitigation bắt buộc: **offsite encrypted backup + restore-to-another-Postgres runbook** (chống concentration risk). Cost planning (record Rev 2 — Day-1 baseline + trigger-based): **`$120–135/mo` Day-1 baseline** (2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker × 1 GiB + DO PG HA `$60.90` + Cloudflare Free + Sentry Developer + KMS `$1–3` + logs/R2 `$0–5` + egress `$0–8`) — **money-safe nhờ non-negotiables + read-isolation controls** (projection-role, bounded pool, reserved budget, CDN, circuit-breaker), KHÔNG cần read-replica/WAF-Pro/Sentry-Team Day-1. Add-on đó = **trigger-based upgrade** khi telemetry chạm ngưỡng. Ephemeral staging `$8–18` → prod+staging `$128–153`. **Caveat:** deferral chỉ an toàn khi read-isolation controls ship thật + trigger được giám sát. (thay số Tier `$202–254` cũ; chi tiết deployment-infra §9 + record §5)
docs/standards/decisions.md-128->
docs/standards/decisions.md-129-> **2026-07-13 (reopen):** Phân tích trong `../dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md` (nay rev 5: rev 2 factual analysis + rev 3 governance + rev 4 fact/cost closure + rev 5 read-isolation/portability closure) đã mở rộng provider/network gate. Chi tiết/nguồn ở brief; tóm tắt canonical dưới đây.

docs/standards/decisions.md-140-  - **Supabase** — Pro và Team **rớt uptime-SLA gate**. Team từ `$599` có AWS PrivateLink nhưng không uptime SLA; SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom, còn auto-failover phải contract-confirm. Không cost-comparable với finalist Day-1.
docs/standards/decisions.md-141-  - **Self-host / Railway-HA** — biến thể self-host (Patroni…), **không** Day-1 (hệ tiền + solo dev): tiết kiệm hạ tầng bị xoá bởi chi phí lao động/rủi ro.
docs/standards/decisions.md-142-- **Sửa dữ kiện so với lần thu hẹp trước:** (1) Neon HA **không** Scale-gated (multi-AZ mọi plan; Scale = SLA/PrivateLink/IP-allow); (2) Supabase Pro/Team không có uptime SLA; Team từ `$599` chỉ thêm PrivateLink, SLA ở Enterprise custom và auto-failover cần contract confirmation; (3) private-networking **chưa** được giải quyết bởi "Fly private networking" (khác cloud).
docs/standards/decisions.md-143-- **Tiêu chí:** auto-failover, SLA, posture network, RLS (Postgres core ở mọi provider), pooler/direct-conn cho pg-boss/`LISTEN`, chi phí *dự đoán được*, độ trễ Singapore, maintainability team nhỏ, **portability (chỉ Postgres thuần)**.
docs/standards/decisions.md-144-- **Hệ quả:** RPO/RTO chốt sau provision + restore drill; benchmark tải trước khi cam kết chi phí (đặc biệt Neon consumption). Không đổi domain model.
docs/standards/decisions.md:145:- **✅ Đã chốt (2026-07-16):** QĐ-1 = **(c) co-locate DO**; QĐ-2 = **DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG). **Còn lại TRƯỚC khi build infra slice:** (1) **§7.1 egress-IP gate** (verify SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger fixed-outbound-IP); (2) production acceptance **§7.6** (auto-failover drill · PITR restore drill · 4 DB roles · SSE reconnect · pg-boss no-dup · budget alerts); (3) benchmark cost ở staging trước procurement.
docs/standards/decisions.md-146-- **Chi tiết:** **`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` (final ✅)** · `../dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md` (rev 5, phân tích/nguồn) · `deployment-infra.md §2/§6/§9 (DD-04)`.

docs/standards/decisions.md:359:## DD-30 — Provider onboarding: APIpay enterprise auto-provision per-host · ✅ Chốt
docs/standards/decisions.md-360-
docs/standards/decisions.md:361:- **Bối cảnh:** Onboarding hiện tại (`feature_connector_onboarding`, Draft) = mỗi host **BYO** (SePay OAuth / ApiPay api_key). Để **launch nhanh**, dùng **quan hệ enterprise với APIpay** ở cấp platform để **auto-provision** webhook/bank-account cho từng host (khỏi bắt host tự OAuth/nhập key).
docs/standards/decisions.md-362-- **Quyết định (chốt):** Platform giữ **APIpay enterprise credential**; onboarding host = platform gọi APIpay enterprise API đăng ký **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/:token`. **Tiền vẫn về tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host** — **money-custody KHÔNG đổi**, giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn theo `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**).
docs/standards/decisions.md-363-- **Lý do:** Giảm ma sát onboarding (self-serve chậm/khó với host phổ thông); tận dụng enterprise để auto-register. Vì không đảo money-custody nên **không** đụng hard rule / rủi ro pháp lý trung gian thanh toán.
docs/standards/decisions.md-364-- **Hệ quả:**
docs/standards/decisions.md:365:  - `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (Draft — sửa được): thêm path **"APIpay enterprise auto-provision"** (platform credential, per-host account/webhook), giữ BYO như fallback; provider vẫn `apipay`; verify HMAC `ApiPay-Signature` per-host secret không đổi.
docs/standards/decisions.md-366-  - **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential platform = tài sản KMS; lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → cần rotation + audit riêng. Cần điều khoản enterprise (giới hạn số account/webhook, phí) từ APIpay.
docs/standards/decisions.md-367-  - **KHÔNG** đụng ledger/reconcile core — chỉ đường onboarding/provisioning.

docs/standards/decisions.md-491-## DD-41 — Field mô tả + gating cho `sessions` · ✅ Chốt (2026-07-13, owner)
docs/standards/decisions.md-492-
docs/standards/decisions.md-493-> Phân tích đầy đủ: `../dd-41-session-fields-gating-analysis.md`. Chi tiết SQL/enforce + player-attribute sources → **slice riêng** khi build (không nhồi vào DD).
docs/standards/decisions.md-494-
docs/standards/decisions.md:495:- **Bối cảnh:** Đối chiếu 6 màn hình *Create Meet / Create Game* của 2 app tham khảo (2026-07-11) với `feature_session_management` + `sessions` schema (`packages/db/schema.ts`): `sessions` hiện **KHÔNG có tên kèo (`title`) lẫn `notes`**, chỉ có **1 field `skill_level`** (text gợi ý — không phải range, không enforce khi join), **không** có `session_type`/format, **không** gender/age restriction, **không** cờ instructions. 2 app kia đều có: Meet name (100 ký tự) + Notes, Social/Class/Singles + Regular/**Coaching**/**Tournament**, Min/Max Level + "Must Have" (strict), Gender/Age restriction, BYOE + Cost-Shared + free-text.
docs/standards/decisions.md-496-- **Quyết định (owner):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-497-  - **Mô tả:** `title` (public+moderated), `notes` (nội bộ), **`session_type` = MỘT field** `social|class|coaching|tournament|singles` *(⚠️ trộn 2 trục — `singles`=format vs intent; nâng 2 trục sau **cần backfill+đổi contract**, KHÔNG "no breakage")*, `byoe`, **`cost_shared` DESCRIPTIVE-ONLY** (KHÔNG đổi công thức `price_amount`/charge; phí biến động/chia-sau = **money slice riêng**), `instructions` (nội bộ).
docs/standards/decisions.md-498-  - **Gating = SESSION POLICY metadata (≠ player gender/DOB).** Enforce ở **shared `evaluateSessionEligibility`** gọi từ **canonical `addMemberToSession`** (TDD) **trước mọi roster mutation** — phủ cross-club/member/host-add/invite (KHÔNG chỉ CCJ). Fields: `level_min_rank`/`level_max_rank`+`level_strict` (rank DD-08), `gender_restriction`, `age_min`/`age_max`. Áp cả personal tenant (DD-46).
docs/standards/decisions.md-499-  - **Decision table (self-attested) — 9 error codes literal:** eligible→join; ineligible→`LEVEL_NOT_ELIGIBLE`/`GENDER_RESTRICTED`/`AGE_RESTRICTED` (level non-strict=advisory); **missing→`LEVEL_ATTRIBUTE_REQUIRED`/`GENDER_ATTRIBUTE_REQUIRED`/`AGE_ATTRIBUTE_REQUIRED`**; **consent-denied→`GENDER_CONSENT_REQUIRED`/`AGE_CONSENT_REQUIRED`** (privacy choice, ≠ missing; level không có consent); **decrypt/KMS-fail→`ATTRIBUTE_UNAVAILABLE`** (infra retry, KHÔNG prompt); `gender undisclosed` vs male/female→`GENDER_RESTRICTED`. **Age tính tại `sessions.starts_at` theo `sessions.timezone`.** Chặn transition open/public (cho draft) nếu source chưa khả dụng. Approval: pre-check + re-check trong transaction.
docs/standards/decisions.md-500-  - **Consent source (PIN):** phân biệt missing vs consent-denied cần **consent state per thuộc tính** (`persons.gender_consent`/`dob_consent` `not_set|granted|denied`; storage → user_settings slice) — `gender_enc`/`dob_enc` NULL không đủ.

docs/standards/decisions.md-544-
docs/standards/decisions.md-545-> Phân tích đầy đủ: `../dd-46-skip-club-analysis.md`. **Chốt: Option B — personal tenant** (phase-1).
docs/standards/decisions.md-546-
docs/standards/decisions.md-547-- **Bối cảnh:** app tham khảo có "Skip Club"/Create Game không cần club → kèo cá nhân. Plexco: `sessions.tenant_id NOT NULL` phổ quát (26+ bảng tenant-scoped) = xương sống RLS + money (HR1).
docs/standards/decisions.md-548-- **CẤM (vĩnh viễn):** session không tenant / `tenant_id` nullable → phá HR1 (RLS keyed tenant_id), HR2 (tiền/PII không tường), HR4 (claim tiền). **Loại tuyệt đối** bất kể option.
docs/standards/decisions.md:549:- **Quyết định (owner): Option B — "personal tenant".** User chọn **không-gian-cá-nhân / Skip Club** (bất kể đã thuộc/own club khác) → **auto-provision một personal tenant** (nền, logical free tier, owner = chính person, `tenants.kind='personal'`). Session gắn tenant đó → **`tenant_id NOT NULL` + RLS + money model NGUYÊN VẸN**. Tái dùng flow tạo tenant sẵn có (WAO-UC-2) + free plan (không sinh invoice).
docs/standards/decisions.md-550-- **Contract đã PIN (khi build):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-551-  - **Schema fail-closed:** `tenants.kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'club' CHECK (kind IN ('club','personal'))` + `owner_person_id uuid REFERENCES persons(id)` + `CHECK ((kind='personal') = (owner_person_id IS NOT NULL))` + **partial UNIQUE INDEX** `ON tenants(owner_person_id) WHERE kind='personal'` (1/person; `UNIQUE...WHERE` không phải table constraint hợp lệ → dùng index).
docs/standards/decisions.md-552-  - **Provision = SHARED CORE:** live **`tenant_provision`** (`0012:256`)/TS **`provisionTenant`** đã tạo tenants+**owner membership**+web-owner tenant_admins+free sub. Personal **tái dùng core**, wrapper **`tenant_get_or_create_personal`**/**`getOrCreatePersonalTenant`** với `name` internal non-PII + slug opaque deterministic + `kind='personal'`+`owner_person_id`; owner membership **vẫn tạo**. Resolve canonical Person + `status='active'` + **lock `persons` row `FOR UPDATE` cùng merge** (không advisory); idempotent.
docs/standards/decisions.md-553-  - **Marketplace (alias ở owner membership):** `host_display_name` ← `memberships.display_name` (Moderated). Owner **CÓ** membership → **public alias = `owner membership.display_name`** (không tạo nguồn mới). Fail-closed: alias trống → **KHÔNG project public**. **CẤM** `person.display_name` thô. `club_display_name` NOT NULL = alias.
docs/standards/decisions.md-554-  - **Money/participant (GIỮ NGUYÊN đường tiền):** personal = tenant đầy đủ → join tạo **guest membership + player + charge**; reconcile/pay_code y hệt. Personal meet **được** có phí.
docs/standards/decisions.md-555-  - **Availability:** Skip Club dùng được **bất kể** người tạo đã thuộc/own club khác (keyed theo person).
docs/standards/decisions.md-556-  - **Merge (DD-01) — decision table:** chỉ loser có personal → re-parent `owner_person_id`; cả hai có → **loser personal → `kind='club'` + clear owner TRƯỚC** (không update vào unique collision). **`tenant_admins` dedupe** predicate đúng scope: `platform='web' AND platform_user_lookup_hash = loser_person_id::text → canonical::text` (Zalo/Messenger dùng provider hash — không đụng); canonical đã có web-admin cùng tenant → **giữ role cao nhất** (`owner>admin>co_admin`) trước khi deactivate dup. ⚠️ hash = UUID text **không FK** (`schema.ts:304`); `identity-model:213` chưa xử lý.
docs/standards/decisions.md-557-  - **Scope:** DD-46 = authority **kiến trúc**. Chi tiết impl (SQL `tenant_get_or_create_personal`, decision table merge đầy đủ, tests concurrency) → **tách design-probe/slice riêng** khi build (`feature_multi_tenant`/`feature_identity`), KHÔNG edge-patch DD.
docs/standards/decisions.md-558-  - **Billing:** personal khoá **logical free tier** (phụ thuộc **DD-29 migration** `free`; live `free_web`); không invoice. Onboarding bỏ "đặt tên club"; owner=self.
docs/standards/decisions.md-559-  - **Guards:** RLS đầy đủ; **rate-limit** `getOrCreatePersonalTenant`.
docs/standards/decisions.md:560:- **Hệ quả / propagation (khi build):** cập nhật `feature_multi_tenant(.md/_tech)` (`tenants.kind` + personal tenant) + `feature_web_auth_onboarding` (auto-provision path) + đánh dấu điểm chạm `feature_identity`/`feature_marketplace_discovery`/`feature_billing_lifecycle`. **Mở khoá cụm session-model** (DD-41/42/43/44/45 build thẳng trên `tenant_id NOT NULL`, personal = một loại tenant). **Không** sửa spec Reviewed tới khi build (đây là DD ✅ nên được sửa theo checklist).

docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-13-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-14-## 1. Mô tả
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-15-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md:16:Cho host mới authenticate, tạo tenant và connect integrations. Readiness matrix đã pin: **required per tenant** = club config (hoặc personal-tenant auto-provision DD-46) + bank verified + first session created; **optional/non-blocking** = Zalo bot và tenant Messenger Page. Bank step mặc định dùng **ApiPay enterprise auto-provision** (DD-30, không nhập platform key); ApiPay BYO key chỉ là fallback do owner chọn. **Messenger central Page** là release gate cấp platform, không phải step per tenant (DD-65).
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-17-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-18-### Bất biến
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-19-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-20-- Google OIDC **PHẢI** verify chữ ký bằng provider key, exact `iss`/`aud`, `state`/`nonce`/PKCE, `exp`/`iat` và `email_verified === true`; không trust returned fields alone.
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-21-- Facebook Login chỉ bật cho non-test user khi exact App Review/Business Verification/permission evidence đã được ghi nhận; code exchange + subject verification chạy server-side. Subject web-auth là stable app-scoped subject của đúng Meta App, **không phải** Messenger Page-scoped PSID và không auto-link với PSID.

docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-28-### 1.1 Surface entry points (web-first vs bot-first)
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-29-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-30-Connector connect (bank/Zalo) **chỉ chạy trên web** — không dán secret/OAuth trong khung chat (token lưu lại lịch sử chat = lộ, vi phạm BL-SEC-1; OAuth cần browser). Hai điểm vào, dùng chung endpoint `/api/onboarding/*`:
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-31-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-32-- **Web-first:** host đăng nhập plexco.app → chạy wizard; Page riêng Messenger có thể skip vì central Page đã dùng được.
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md:33:- **Bot-first:** host mở bot CLB qua **Zalo** hoặc **Facebook Messenger**; phần không nhạy cảm (tên CLB, môn, giá, session đầu) hỏi ngay trong chat; bank selection/OAuth/BYO credential và token kênh đều đi qua **magic-link 1 lần** → host hoàn tất trên web → quay lại bot.
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-34-
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md:35:> **Zalo** = BYO Bot Token per-CLB. **Messenger** = một platform App + central Page; optional tenant Page qua FB Login → chọn Page. Central Page readiness do platform ops sở hữu, không lặp trong từng tenant wizard.
docs/features/feature_web_auth_onboarding.md-36-

Target providers là `{google,email,facebook,zalo}` nhưng live DB chưa có platform CHECK; `persons` vẫn là anchor và Zalo Login vẫn gated DD-14. Chi tiết: decisions DD-13/14/33 + identity-model §7.

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-1-# 💳 DD-02 Deep Analysis — `pay_code` (rev 6)
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-2-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:3:> **Mục đích:** chốt **contract pay_code** đúng với core đã có + provider-agnostic (ApiPay primary, SePay fallback).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:4:> **Ngày:** 2026-07-13 (rev 6) · **Canonical:** `packages/core/src/matching.ts` (`extractPayCode`/`countPayCodes`) · `packages/core/src/payments-logic.ts` (`classifyIncoming`/`classifyCoverage`) · `packages/db/schema.ts` (memberships/tenants) · `feature_payment_reconcile.md` (edge #8/#9) + `business-rules.md` (BL-PAY-2..7) · `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (ApiPay/SePay, CO-OQ-6) · `identity-model.md` (merge) · DD-30 · AUDIT.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-5-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-6-> ⚠️ **REV 6** (xem §0.1): (a) **1 nguồn prefix** = `pay_prefix_registry` (bỏ `tenants.pay_prefix`, one-current-per-tenant); (b) **prefix IMMUTABLE sau first issuance** + **choke-point atomic** phát-code↔đổi-prefix (§6.1); (c) **full-issued code định nghĩa hình thức** = `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp (§3.4) → retired-chưa-lock không tạo false issued; (d) memo-level precedence + `planIncomingAllocations()` (unknown-fee đầu vs sau).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-21-  | Không hard-token, **đúng 1 distinct own code** | resolve membership → ledger §4 |
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-22-  | **Chỉ `none`, không code evidence** (vd `PAYMENT`) | **name-fallback** |
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-23-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-24-  *Lặp cùng một own code hai lần = một distinct code. `none` chỉ dẫn tới name-fallback khi memo **không** chứa hard-token nào.*
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-25-- **Amount/allocation = INVARIANT:** pay_code chỉ **resolve MEMBERSHIP** (ai). **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (§4) dựng plan — **auto: `exact` / `over` / `under`(FIFO partial) / unknown-fee-PHÍA-SAU (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; **manual: unknown-fee-ĐẦU / `already-paid`**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:26:- **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở **Plexco core**. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30 ✅) — parse `data.content` (không có field code bóc sẵn). **SePay = optional fallback** — `code` chỉ là **evidence bổ sung, KHÔNG canonical**; `code` ↔ content/prefix/tenant bất đồng → **fail-closed → unmatched**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-27-- **Giữ webhook nhận cả giao dịch KHÔNG có code** (để name-based fallback chạy tiếp). **Không** bật `skip_if_no_code` ở tầng provider.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-28-- **Phase:** pay_code = **P4** (BL-PAY-3). `decisions.md` DD-02 **đã sửa P3→P4** (2026-07-13).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-277-- SePay `code` là evidence; **core không được phụ thuộc** vào nó. `providerCode` có nhưng **không khớp** parser/content/prefix/tenant → **unmatched** (fail-closed).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-278-- Options như `is_verify_payment`, `skip_if_no_code` **chỉ thuộc SePay adapter**, **không** vào core DD-02. **Không** bật `skip_if_no_code` (sẽ khiến giao dịch thiếu code không tới Plexco, phá name-based fallback).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-279-- **`pay_prefix_registry`** là **canonical của Plexco**, KHÔNG phải "cấu hình SePay".
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:280:- ApiPay + SePay chạy **cùng fixtures** (§11).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-304-**⏳ CODE/MIGRATION còn lại (khi build P4) — impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts` (111/111 xanh):**
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-305-1. **Parser:** `payCodeRe` + `-?` + **`discoverPayCodeCandidates`** (enumerate mọi split, không loại `skipCodes` pre-resolve); **resolver narrow SECURITY DEFINER** `own|foreign|none` (full-issued = `locked_at NOT NULL` + `pay_code` match). Wire vào reconcile production.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-306-2. **Ledger:** **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (§4). `classifyCoverage` để dành combo.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-307-3. **Migration:** **`pay_prefix_registry`** (PK `prefix`, state, `locked_at`, one-current-per-tenant, **không** `tenants.pay_prefix`; choke-point atomic §6.1) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code` (format + `[A-Z]`) + **`bank_transactions.provider_code_enc`** (evidence) + **`provider_connections` CHECK shape đầy đủ per (provider, auth_mode)** (spec đã có).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-308-   > ⚠️ **Backfill guard (bắt buộc khi migrate):** query DB **thật** xem có `memberships.pay_code IS NOT NULL` không. Code search hiện **không thấy production writer**, nhưng nếu DB đã có dữ liệu → **HALT**: map prefix **thủ công** per-tenant + set `locked_at` cho từng prefix đã phát. **KHÔNG** suy prefix từ `slug` (sai namespace → resolve nhầm). CHECK/registry chỉ bật sau khi backfill xong.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-309-4. **Merge code:** recognition follow `merged_into_membership_id` (feature_identity merge path). **Provider-code cross-check** (SePay `provider_code` ↔ candidate/content/prefix/tenant → mismatch = hard-unmatched).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:310:5. **Provider verify (build):** verify webhook + dedupe (`UNIQUE(tenant,provider,external_id)` — BL-PAY-4) + amount, cho **cả** ApiPay lẫn SePay.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:311:   > ⚠️ **ApiPay header/payload schema chưa công khai đầy đủ** — trang chính thức mới xác nhận có webhook/API + "verify chữ ký nếu hỗ trợ". Field (`data.content`/`amount`/`transactionId`/`bankAccountPublicId`/`ApiPay-Signature`) §7 phải **REVERIFY khi build**. Positioning ApiPay-primary (DD-30) không đổi.

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-315-## 10. Quyết định (đã chốt) + việc thực thi
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-316-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-317-1. **Độ dài suffix = 4** (owner 2026-07-13) — contract duy nhất, không còn tuỳ chọn 5.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-318-2. **Prefix:** nguồn duy nhất = **`pay_prefix_registry`** (prefix PK, state, `locked_at`, **one-current-per-tenant**; **không** cột `tenants.pay_prefix`); retired không tái dụng. **Prefix IMMUTABLE sau first issuance**; **issuance và pre-lock change dùng CÙNG `FOR UPDATE` choke-point** (§6.1). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:319:3. **Bất biến:** recognition qua resolver **`own|foreign|none`** — **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`; dash-token lạ → hard); **memo-level precedence** (hard-token thắng name-fallback; own-unissued/foreign/error/>1-candidate → HARD unmatched; ≥2 own → unmatched; đúng 1 → resolve; chỉ none → name-fallback); **allocation qua `planIncomingAllocations()`** (auto exact/over/under-partial + unknown-fee-phía-sau; manual unknown-fee-đầu/already-paid); provider-agnostic (ApiPay primary, SePay code = evidence); `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện; **`merged` → follow tombstone**.
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:320:4. **Propagation:** SPEC **đã làm** (phase P4, connector ApiPay ✅ primary + `auth_mode='enterprise'` + CO-OQ-6 đóng, merge tombstone ở identity-model/_tech, reconcile §4/§8) — xem §9. **Còn lại = code/migration khi build P4** (parser/resolver/`planIncomingAllocations`/`pay_prefix_registry`).
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-321-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-322-**Đã ghi canonical (2026-07-13):** `decisions.md` DD-02 → ✅ + phase P4 + changelog v1.22/v1.23; spec propagation (identity-model/_tech, connector .md/_tech + `auth_mode='enterprise'`, reconcile §4/§8) — xem §9. **Còn lại = code/migration khi build P4** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`).

docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-325-
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md:326:## 11. Test fixtures bắt buộc (ApiPay + SePay cùng bộ)
docs/dd-02-paycode-analysis.md-327-`THANH TOAN` (không nhầm mã `THANH`) · **`PAYMENT` khi `PAY` KHÔNG own → `none` → name-fallback** · **`PAYMENT` khi `PAY` LÀ own current prefix → `MENT` unissued → HARD unmatched** · **own prefix + code UNISSUED `BMT7Q2B` → HARD unmatched** · **`BMTRANS` (skipCodes noise, unresolved squashed) → `none`** · **`BMT-RANS` (dash + skipCodes) → HARD** · **token trùng skipCodes nhưng full-issued → resolve theo ownership (issued thắng skipCodes)** · **foreign full-issued code trong memo (vd `... ABC7Q2A ...`, ABC là tenant khác đã lock) → discover + resolve → HARD unmatched** (không bỏ sót do chỉ anchor own-prefix) · `ABC-7Q2A` dash prefix lạ → hard-unmatched · bare code (reject deterministic) · no-token → name-fallback · **`BMT-7Q2A` + `BMT7Q2A` đều nhận** · provider/raw mismatch (→unmatched) · **lowercase / toàn-số DB insert → CHECK reject** · **`left` còn open charge → nhận diện** · `left` hết nợ → already-paid/unmatched · **`merged` → follow tombstone** (QR cũ chạy) · nhiều code (→unmatched) · `planIncomingAllocations`: exact→auto · over→auto+overpay · **under→auto FIFO partial** (charge `partial`, không notify paid) · **unknown-fee PHÍA SAU (100k known → unknown, trả 150k) → allocate 100k debt đầu, giữ 50k unallocated** · **unknown-fee ĐẦU → toàn bộ unmatched** · already-paid→unmatched · **retired prefix KHÔNG cấp lại** · **2 tenant claim cùng prefix đồng thời → PK cho đúng 1 tx thắng** · one-current-per-tenant enforce · **concurrent first-issuance vs prefix-change → đúng 1 thắng (§6.1)** · **đổi BMT→ABC TRƯỚC first issuance → phát `ABC-9K2P`: `BMT9K2P` (squashed, BMT `locked_at IS NULL`) = `none`/name-fallback, nhưng `BMT-9K2P` (dash) = HARD unmatched** · **retired prefix có `locked_at` (từng phát) + exact suffix → resolver công nhận** · >1 candidate → hard-unmatched · cross-tenant · dirty memo.

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-19-|---|---|
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-20-| Zalo | Tạo bot → dán token → **tự vào Zalo platform dán webhook URL** `{api_base}/webhook/zalo/{token}` (`channels/zalo/README.md` §6) |
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-21-| SePay | Nhập credential → **tự vào SePay dashboard tạo webhook** trỏ về Plexco (`feature_web_auth_onboarding` step bank) |

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-23-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-24-Bước "copy URL → mò vào dashboard bên thứ ba → dán đúng chỗ → set trigger/secret" là điểm rơi onboarding lớn nhất cho non-tech user, và cũng là nguồn lỗi "webhook drift".
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-25-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-26-**Đề xuất:** Đảo nguyên tắc — **owner chỉ xác nhận tài khoản nhận tiền hoặc cấp credential thuộc về họ; Plexco tự đăng ký webhook bằng API**. Mức độ chắc chắn (kiểm chứng dashboard/docs thật ngày 2026-06-20):
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-27-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-28-- **SePay** — ✅ *verified*. Có **OAuth2** (scopes `bank-account:read`, `transaction:read`, `webhook:read|write|delete`) + `POST /api/v1/webhooks` (contract đầy đủ — §4.2) + `GET /api/v1/transactions` + **giả lập giao dịch** → "Kết nối SePay" 1 nút, không dán token.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:29:- **ApiPay** — **phase-1 primary = enterprise auto-provision (DD-30):** host không nhập platform credential; Plexco provision bank-account/webhook riêng cho host, tiền vẫn về TK host. API-key BYO qua `GET /v2/client/banks` + `POST /v1/client/webhooks` là fallback explicit. Standard webhook API đã verified; request shape/terms của enterprise phải được xác nhận trước production.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-30-- **Zalo** — 🟡 *candidate, blocked by sandbox confirmation (CO-OQ-1)*. Pattern token + `getMe`/`setWebhook` xuất hiện trong các SDK Zalo Bot cộng đồng, **nhưng chưa kiểm chứng endpoint/tham số chính thức** trên tier Zalo Bot mà CLB dùng. **Không** đưa vào triển khai cho tới khi confirm sandbox; trước đó giữ luồng nhập tay hiện có.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-31-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:32:**Kết quả:** 3 flow rối → 1 pattern "Kết nối" thống nhất, kết thúc bằng tín hiệu xanh "đã nhận giao dịch/tin nhắn thử". SePay/ApiPay standard webhook auto-register bỏ bước dán URL; enterprise ApiPay chỉ bật sau khi provider contract chứng minh request shape + replay safety; Zalo theo sau khi gỡ block.

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:47:| ApiPay | standard/BYO docs: `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication`, `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/banks`, `https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/webhooks`; DD-30 là authority cho primary path | Standard/BYO: `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`; banks `/v2/client/banks`; webhooks `/v1/client/webhooks`; `ApiPay-Signature` HMAC raw body | enterprise request/response + exact replay mechanism + terms/SLA; standard sandbox status/error mapping |

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-50----
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-51-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-52-## 1. Mô tả
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-53-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:54:Cung cấp một trải nghiệm "Kết nối" (Connect) thống nhất cho 3 connector. ApiPay phase-1 dùng enterprise auto-provision nên host chỉ chọn/xác nhận TK nhận tiền, không nhập platform credential; SePay dùng OAuth consent; Zalo và ApiPay-BYO fallback mới nhận credential thuộc về owner. Việc đăng ký webhook về Plexco và xác minh hoạt động do hệ thống tự làm.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-55-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-56-### Bất biến
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-57-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-58-- **B1 — Routing theo opaque token, KHÔNG theo nội dung payload.** Tenant luôn suy ra từ `:token` trong path → `*.webhook_token → tenant_id`, rồi mới verify `account_fingerprint`/account khớp. Ref: **BL-PAY-6**, **BL-ID-6**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-59-- **B2 — Secret at-rest qua KMS, write-only.** Mọi token/secret (`bot_token_enc`, `webhook_secret_enc`, OAuth tokens, PKCE verifier) mã hoá envelope (AES-GCM); không log; không trả lại sau khi lưu; có reset path. Ref: **BL-SEC-1**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-60-- **B3 — Verify webhook timing-safe trên RAW body.** Sai chữ ký/timestamp → 401, không persist payload nghiệp vụ, security log không chứa secret. SePay production dùng HMAC-SHA256 trên `{unix_timestamp}.{raw_body}`, timestamp freshness ±300s và constant-time compare. API key chỉ explicit fallback/sandbox. Ref: **BL-SEC-2**, AUDIT B-webhook.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-61-- **B4 — RLS fail-closed.** Mọi bảng tenant-scoped ENABLE + FORCE RLS theo `app.tenant_id`. Riêng `oauth_transactions` là bảng bootstrap trước-tenant cho OAuth callback, không đọc trực tiếp qua tenant RLS; chỉ truy cập qua SECURITY DEFINER function lookup-by-state single-use. Ref: **BL-TEN-1**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-62-- **B5 — Tự động hoá KHÔNG nới lỏng bảo mật.** Auto-register chỉ chạy trong context tenant đã auth (TenantOwner), idempotent, chịu rate limit **BL-SEC-5**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:63:- **B6 — Auto-register là tiện ích, không phải điểm chết.** ApiPay enterprise không khả dụng/không phủ bank → hiện CTA **BYO API key fallback**; provider webhook API vẫn fail mới tới rescue nhập URL tay. Không tự downgrade `auth_mode` hoặc chuyển sang BYO mà chưa có hành động rõ ràng của owner.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-64-
