docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-370-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-371-**Fallback:** chưa kịp đăng ký OAuth partner (CO-OQ-2) → owner dán **API token** có scope webhook cần dùng → vẫn `POST /api/v1/webhooks`. Inbound auth vẫn mặc định HMAC; fallback API-key phải explicit và lưu `webhook_auth_mode`, không downgrade động. Cuối cùng mới tới luồng dán URL tay (B6).
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-372-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:373:### 4.3 ApiPay — enterprise auto-provision PRIMARY; BYO API key FALLBACK
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-374-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:375:#### 4.3.1 Primary — enterprise auto-provision (DD-30 ✅ · DD-02)
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-376-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:377:Platform giữ **1 credential ApiPay enterprise** và tự động provision per-host; host không phải tạo/copy API key:
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-378-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-379-- **Onboarding host:** host chọn/xác nhận TK ngân hàng của chính mình theo flow provider, rồi web gọi `POST /api/onboarding/apipay/provision` với `Idempotency-Key`, `bankSelectionRef` opaque và consent. Request **không** nhận platform secret, accessKey/secretKey hay số TK thô.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-380-- **Provision:** backend resolve `bankSelectionRef` đúng tenant/owner, dùng enterprise credential trong platform KMS để tạo/bind **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/{webhookToken}`; response chỉ trả Plexco IDs + `registered`, không trả credential/webhook secret.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:381:- **Money-custody KHÔNG đổi:** tiền vẫn về **tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host**; Plexco **không** thu hộ → giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` + 5 HARD RULES ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**); verify `ApiPay-Signature` HMAC per-host **không đổi**.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:382:- **Fallback có chủ đích:** chỉ hiện CTA "Dùng ApiPay key riêng" khi enterprise không khả dụng/không phủ bank hoặc host muốn tự quản key. Không tự đổi `auth_mode='enterprise'` sang `api_key`.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-383-- **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential là tài sản **KMS cấp platform** (không per-tenant); lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → rotation + audit riêng + rate-limit provisioning. `provider_connections.auth_mode='enterprise'` phân biệt rõ với `api_key`.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:384:- **Production gate:** xác nhận điều khoản + request/response thực tế của enterprise ApiPay (giới hạn account/webhook, phí, SLA) và một replay mechanism chứng minh được: provider idempotency key **hoặc** exact lookup/reuse bằng deterministic provision ref. Không có một trong hai → `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE`, không gọi create mù sau timeout và không suy từ standard BYO API.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-385-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:386:> Nguồn authority: **DD-30** (`../standards/decisions.md`). Provider vẫn `apipay`; chỉ khác ai cấp credential và ai provision. KHÔNG đụng ledger/reconcile core. Internal endpoint chi tiết ở `BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md` §3.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-387-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:388:#### 4.3.2 Fallback — BYO API key + auto-register (standard API ✅ verified)

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-391-

docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-590-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-591-## 10. Acceptance Criteria
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-592-
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-593-- [ ] Chủ CLB kết nối SePay chỉ bằng OAuth consent (không dán token); webhook tạo tự động theo contract §4.2.2.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:594:- [ ] ApiPay phase-1 primary dùng enterprise auto-provision: host không nhập platform credential, tiền vẫn về TK riêng của host; BYO 2-key chỉ là fallback và TK `PENDING` hiển thị đúng.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-595-- [ ] `/apipay/provision` retry/crash không tạo provider resource trùng; enterprise path fail-closed OFF nếu provider chưa chứng minh idempotency key hoặc exact deterministic lookup/reuse.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md:596:- [ ] Luồng chính SePay/ApiPay không bắt owner dán webhook URL; manual URL chỉ còn là fallback khi provider API fail hoặc Zalo chưa gỡ CO-OQ-1.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-597-- [ ] Mỗi connector hiện trạng thái theo vocabulary §3.1 (`registered` → `active`) và xác minh được bằng event/giả lập.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-598-- [ ] Early verified event không bị connect finalization hạ `active→registered`; transition dùng CAS decision table và chỉ emit `connector_verified` một lần.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-599-- [ ] Verify webhook timing-safe **trên raw body**; routing theo token (BL-PAY-6); RLS fail-closed (BL-TEN-1).
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-600-- [ ] OAuth callback không phụ thuộc cookie; chống replay bằng state 1 lần + PKCE.
docs/features/feature_connector_onboarding.md-601-- [ ] Provider API fail có fallback, không chặn cứng onboarding (B6).

docs/features/feature_payment_reconcile.md-100-| # | Case | Hành vi bắt buộc |
docs/features/feature_payment_reconcile.md-101-|---|---|---|
docs/features/feature_payment_reconcile.md:102:| 1 | Provider authentication sai | 401 trước parse/persist: SePay production requires `X-SePay-Signature: sha256=<hex>` over `{X-SePay-Timestamp}.{raw_body}`, timestamp ±300s, constant-time; HMAC failure never downgrades to API key. Explicit SePay fallback accepts only `Authorization: Apikey <key>`. ApiPay separately verifies `ApiPay-Signature = HMAC-SHA256(raw_body, per-webhook secret)`. Không log secret/raw body. |

docs/features/feature_payment_reconcile.md-105-| 4 | Duplicate đồng thời | UNIQUE `(tenant, provider, external_id)`; một durable job |
docs/features/feature_payment_reconcile.md-106-| 5 | Process chết sau ACK | Job đã durable; retry tiếp tục từ `pending/unmatched` |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-177-| 5 | Security | Secret/token rò ra log | KMS at-rest, redaction; chỉ lưu `last_error_code` (BL-SEC-1) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-178-| 6 | Concurrency | 2 lần "Kết nối" đồng thời | `UNIQUE(tenant,provider)` + upsert; chỉ 1 webhook đăng ký |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-179-| 7 | Concurrency | Event đến trước khi `registered` commit | Chỉ `connecting` + ciphertext được verify; hợp lệ → CAS `connecting→active`. Register finalization CAS `connecting→registered` và preserve `active`; thiếu ciphertext/`error|revoked` reject |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-180-| 8 | Concurrency | Replay event nhà cung cấp | Flip Tier 2 idempotent; ingest dedup `(tenant,provider,external_id)` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-181-| 9 | Data Integrity | Provider tạo webhook OK nhưng DB commit fail | Lưu `provider_webhook_id` cùng transaction; lệch → job `connectors/cleanup-orphan-webhook` gọi `DELETE` webhook mồ côi |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:182:| 10 | Data Integrity (ApiPay) | TK `PENDING`/`manualActivationPending` | Không register; `webhook_status='connecting'`; job `connectors/recheck-pending-bank` (≤24h) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-183-| 11 | Data Integrity | Drift — owner đổi URL ở dashboard | `security/webhook-drift` (hourly) → auto re-register; fail → `drift`/alert |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-184-| 12 | Token lifecycle | SePay refresh token hết hạn/refresh fail | `status='error'` + nhắc kết nối lại; **không** ảnh hưởng connection/TK khác |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:185:| 13 | Token lifecycle | ApiPay BYO key bị thu hồi bên ngoài | Delivery fail liên tục → `error` + alert; runbook rotate (enterprise credential có platform-level rotation/audit riêng) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-186-| 14 | Cross-Feature | Gỡ connector | revoke OAuth / `DELETE webhook` → `status='revoked'`; **giữ** lịch sử `bank_transactions` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-187-| 15 | Cross-Feature | `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` (fingerprint trùng tenant khác) | 409, không bind; tránh route nhầm tenant |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-188-| 16 | Abuse | Spam `*/connect` dò key | Rate limit BL-SEC-5 (§3) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-189-| 17 | Security | SePay HMAC stale/future timestamp, bad signature hoặc missing header | 401 trước parse/persist; no auth downgrade |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-190-| 18 | Security | SePay API-key fallback header sai | Chỉ exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` constant-time; reject bare/custom header |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-232-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:233:- `bankSelectionRef` là reference opaque, TTL ngắn, do server cấp sau flow host chọn/xác nhận TK theo cơ chế enterprise đã được ApiPay phê duyệt; nó bound với tenant + owner + bank selection. Nó **không** phải accessKey/secretKey, platform credential, số TK thô hay provider webhook secret. Nếu enterprise contract thực tế không cung cấp được reference tương đương, HALT production mapping và xác nhận lại provider request shape; không tự mở input PII/secret mới.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-234-- Request schema `additionalProperties=false`: reject mọi field credential/secret ngoài shape trên. `consent` phải đúng `true`; resolve ref + authorization + TTL trước provider I/O.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-235-- Handler lock logical `(tenant_id, provider='apipay')`, upsert `provider_connections.auth_mode='enterprise'`, rồi provision/bind bank + webhook. Provider-side replay phải dùng **một cơ chế đã được enterprise contract chứng minh**: stable derived idempotency key nếu provider hỗ trợ, hoặc exact lookup/reuse bằng deterministic provision ref. Không có cơ chế nào → trả `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE`, không gọi create mù sau timeout. Enterprise credential chỉ đọc từ platform KMS, không lưu vào tenant row, response, analytics hay log.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-236-- Provider success + DB commit fail đi qua orphan cleanup §4.2; retry cùng key phải reuse provider resource/response, không tạo bank/webhook thứ hai. Cùng key nhưng body hash khác → `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` trước provider call.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:237:- Tenant đã có compatible enterprise binding → trả resource hiện hữu. Muốn đổi bank hoặc đổi `enterprise ↔ api_key` phải qua replace/revoke flow explicit; endpoint không silently downgrade sang BYO.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:238:- Enterprise unavailable/unsupported chỉ trả error có type để UI hiện CTA **"Dùng ApiPay key riêng"**; BYO endpoints không được gọi tự động.

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:245:| POST | `/webhook/apipay/:token` | `ApiPay-Signature` == HMAC-SHA256(rawBody, `webhook_secret`) | bank_transactions ingest |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-246-| POST | `/webhook/zalo/:token` 🟡 | Narrow endpoint resolve → require ciphertext + `connecting|registered|active|drift` → KMS-decrypt `zalo_bots.webhook_secret_enc` → timing-safe compare `X-Bot-Api-Secret-Token`; `connecting` without ciphertext plus `error|revoked` fail closed; no plaintext fallback | channel event handler |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-270-| 400 | `OAUTH_STATE_INVALID` | "Phiên kết nối hết hạn, thử lại" | state không khớp/đã dùng/hết hạn |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-281-| `apipay/provision` | ✅ | required `(tenant_id, route, Idempotency-Key)` + request hash + persisted canonical response; provider replay dùng verified idempotency key hoặc exact deterministic lookup/reuse; thiếu cả hai thì path fail-closed unavailable; same key/different body → 409 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:282:| `apipay/connect` (BYO), `sepay/oauth/start` | ✅ | upsert `(tenant_id, provider)` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-283-| `oauth/callback` | ✅ | `state` single-use (xóa sau exchange) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-284-| `zalo/connect` 🟡 | ✅ | `(tenant_id)` — `zalo_bots` UNIQUE(tenant) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-285-| Webhook ingest | ✅ | `(tenant, provider, external_id)` (delegate payment ingest) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-286-| Flip Tier 2 | ✅ | CAS `UPDATE ... WHERE status IN ('connecting','registered') RETURNING`; emit analytics only when one row transitions. `active` is idempotent; `drift` is processed without clearing drift; `error|revoked` reject |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-447-### Rollout (khớp product §11)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-448-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:449:1. **M1** — receiver verify 2 tầng + SePay/ApiPay raw-body HMAC; SePay freshness/no-downgrade; flip `active` khi event đầu.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:450:2. **M2** — **ApiPay ENTERPRISE auto-provision per-host = Phase-1 PRIMARY (DD-30 ✅ · DD-02)**: platform giữ 1 credential enterprise, tự provision per-host (không bắt host tự lấy `api_key`); BYO `api_key` là fallback. Xử lý bank `PENDING`. *(rủi ro thấp nhất, giao trước)*
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-451-3. **M3** — SePay OAuth2 (cần partner CO-OQ-2) + state/PKCE + refresh job; fallback token mode; tùy chọn poll `/transactions`.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-452-4. **M4** — Zalo auto `setWebhook` *(chỉ sau khi gỡ CO-OQ-1)*: land KMS backfill-or-disable/drop migration, state+ciphertext decision table and early-event CAS tests before receiver enablement; then drift auto re-register.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-453-5. **M5** — dọn fallback, telemetry, ẩn dần luồng nhập tay.

docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-470-  - `THANH TOAN`; `PAYMENT` (own vs non-own `PAY`); `BMT7Q2B` unissued → hard; foreign full-issued → hard; `ABC-7Q2A` foreign dash → hard; `BMTRANS` noise → `none`; `BMT-RANS` → hard; full-issued token in `skipCodes` → resolve (issued wins); `BMT7Q2A`/`BMT-7Q2A` both accepted; prefix-change-before-first-issuance (`BMT9K2P` none / `BMT-9K2P` hard); unknown-fee 100k→unknown pay 150k → allocate 100k keep 50k; unknown-fee-first → unmatched; concurrent prefix claim → 1 tx wins; lowercase/all-digit CHECK reject; `merged` → follow tombstone.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-471-
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-472-### Integration
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-473-
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-474-- SePay exact-auth fixtures: valid timestamp/raw-body signature passes; reserialized body, missing/malformed `sha256=`, stale/future timestamp or bad HMAC returns 401 before durable row/job. HMAC-configured endpoint never downgrades even when a valid API key is also supplied; explicit fallback accepts only `Authorization: Apikey <key>`.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md:475:- ApiPay exact-auth fixtures use its own raw-body `ApiPay-Signature`; SePay headers do not authenticate ApiPay and vice versa.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-476-- Raw + job commit before ACK; injected crash before commit yields no ACK.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-477-- Injected crash after ACK is recovered by durable job/sweeper.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-478-- Concurrent duplicate webhooks create one transaction.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-479-- Concurrent auto/manual/cash cannot over-allocate.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md:480:- **(DD-02) SePay `provider_code` ↔ content mismatch** (provider bóc code khác với code trong `content`/prefix/tenant) → **hard-unmatched** (fail-closed, không tự chọn bên nào); ApiPay (không có provider code) → chỉ dựa content resolver.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-481-- **Mark-paid (DD-40):** tiền mặt → allocation `source='cash'`+`created_by`, không tạo bank txn giả; CK → allocation `source='manual'` khớp một `bank_transactions` row; enum không có `bank_transfer`; multi-select batch idempotent theo `commandId` (retry không double); DD-25 preview→confirm bắt buộc; markPaid → 1 confirm intent tới player.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-482-- **B1 attested transfer:** before migration → `ATTESTED_TRANSFER_UNSUPPORTED`; after migration → dedicated source, NULL bank txn, actor+opaque evidence; never synthetic bank transaction or `cash|manual` fallback.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-483-- **DD-49 correction:** price up/down append obligation adjustment; refund reversal links original allocation; carry-over rejoin creates no financial row; refunded rejoin creates one idempotent obligation lifecycle; unknown state fails closed. Every assertion reads the canonical balance helper.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-484-- Partial, overpay, multi-charge and multi-person manual allocation totals.
docs/features/BE/feature_payment_reconcile_tech.md-485-- Reversal restores bank remainder/charge status exactly.

 Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
 and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.
 
 ---
 
 ## 0. Target environment model
 
 ```text
 local development
   docker compose + local PostgreSQL
             │
             │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
             ▼
 ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
   production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
   sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
             │
             │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
             ▼
 production on DigitalOcean
   2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
 ```
 
 | Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
 | **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
 | **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
 | **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
 
 Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
 production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
 automatically after the TTL.
 
 ---
 
 ## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
 
 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
-DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. Record
-written evidence for every production dependency before creating the App Spec:
+DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
+table below closes this gate with attributable evidence from current official provider documentation, one row
+per production dependency and its exact Plexco outbound call surface.
 
-| Dependency | Required answer |
-|---|---|
-| SePay | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Inbound webhook verification alone does not imply that it does. |
-| ApiPay | Does enterprise provisioning restrict API callers by fixed source IP? |
-| Zalo Bot | Does the send API require fixed outbound IP? |
-| Meta Messenger | Does the Graph API require fixed outbound IP? |
+| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. The docs' only IP-allowlist page lists SePay's own egress IPs for merchants to allowlist SePay→merchant webhook delivery — the reverse direction — so inbound webhook hardening has no outbound-caller counterpart. |
+| **ApiPay** — backend calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) for DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning and the BYO-key fallback | **NO** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Authentication is fully specified as `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)` with keys issued in the ApiPay dashboard. The entire docs corpus contains no IP-allowlist, IP-registration or caller-IP-restriction concept; the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A-record instructions pointing at ApiPay's own server. |
+| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **NO** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The complete calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL). Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
+| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
+
+<!-- DD04_EGRESS_EVIDENCE_CLOSED -->
+
+**Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE** — all four rows are attributable and all are **NO**.
 
-- All answers **no** → continue with App Platform + VPC.
-- Any answer **yes** → stop. Do not disable the private DB path silently. Activate the bounded Fly+DO
-  fallback or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access and controlled egress.
+- `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review
+  only**; it does not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning. The marker above proves
+  evidence completeness, not that the answer was favorable.
+- A `FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED` result would have blocked the DO App Platform + VPC green path and required a
+  separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access
+  and controlled egress.
+- If a future provider or contract change turns any row **YES**, stop. Do not disable the private DB path
+  silently; re-open this gate and the fallback decision before touching the App Spec.
 
 ### 1.2 Repository preconditions
 
 Before the infra slice runs:
 
 ```bash
 test -f infra/Dockerfile
 test -f infra/entrypoint.sh
 test -f .github/workflows/ci.yml
 test -f infra/app.prod.yaml       # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 test -f infra/app.staging.yaml    # created and reviewed by the infra slice
 ```
 
 If either App Spec is absent, this runbook is a deployment contract only: **do not improvise a console-only
 production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.
 
 ---
 
 ## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
 
 Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
 Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
 
 | Resource | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|
 | App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
 | App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
 | VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
 | PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
 | DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
 | Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
 | Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
 
 Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
 ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.
 
 ---
 
 ## 3. One image, three App Platform components
 
@@ -237,81 +247,82 @@ not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
 
 ## 8. Rollback and recovery
 
 ### Application rollback
 
 - Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
 - Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
 - If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.
 
 ### Database recovery
 
 - Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
 - Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
   ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
 - Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
   migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.
 
 ---
 
 ## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
 
 Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
 
 1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
 2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
 3. runs the selected gates;
 4. exports drill evidence;
 5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
 6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
 
 Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
 silently become an always-on production mirror.
 
 ---
 
 ## 10. Production acceptance checklist
 
 This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
 slice:
 
-- [ ] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence.
+- [x] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — §1.1, checked 2026-07-18, overall result
+      `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE`.
 - [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
 - [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
 - [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.
 - [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` pass rolling-deploy and health tests.
 - [ ] Discovery pool cap, `statement_timeout`, reserved money connection budget, CDN/rate limits and circuit breaker pass load tests.
 - [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.
 - [ ] SSE re-subscribes and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
 - [ ] PITR and off-provider restores meet measured RPO/RTO and reconciliation gates.
 - [ ] KMS rotation and decrypt-failure paths pass.
 - [ ] Ephemeral staging reproduces from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
 - [ ] Load evidence captures RPS/concurrency, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate, egress and projected cost.
 - [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
 - [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.
 
 ---
 
 ## 11. Cost planning
 
 | Environment | Planning envelope |
 |---|---:|
 | Local/integration smoke | `$0–15/month`, depending on whether a shared public callback target is kept |
 | Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |
 | Production Day 1 | **`$120–135/month`** |
 | Production + expected staging | **`$128–153/month`** |
 
 The production baseline includes two `web`, two `discovery`, one worker, DO PostgreSQL primary+standby,
 Cloudflare Free, Sentry Developer, KMS, low-volume logs/offsite backup and egress allowances. Physical read-only
 node, second worker, WAF Pro, Sentry Team and resizing are telemetry-triggered upgrades, not Day-1 requirements.
 
 Host count does not select a resource tier. Scale purchases require staging/production measurements and the
 triggers in DD-04 §5.2.
 
 ---
 
 ## 12. Bounded Fly fallback
 
 `infra/fly.toml` is retained only because DD-04 pre-authorizes fallback `(a)` when a production dependency proves
 that fixed outbound IP is mandatory and App Platform+VPC cannot satisfy it.
 
 Activating fallback requires a recorded gate result and a separate reviewed App/DB network plan:

 ```text
             ▼
 ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
   production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
   sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
             │
             │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
             ▼
 production on DigitalOcean
   2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
 ```
 
 | Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
 | **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
 | **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
 | **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
 
 Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
 production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
 automatically after the TTL.

 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
 DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The

-per production dependency and its exact Plexco outbound call surface.

+and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
+`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
+contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
 
 | Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
-| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
-| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |

 ### 1.2 Repository preconditions
 
 Before the infra slice runs:
 
 ```bash
 test -f infra/Dockerfile
 ```

 production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.

 ## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
 
 Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
 Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
 
 | Resource | Staging | Production |
 |---|---|---|
 | App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
 | App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
 | VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
 | PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
 | DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
 | Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
 | Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
 
 Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
 ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.

 ## 8. Rollback and recovery
 
 ### Application rollback
 
 - Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
 - Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
 - If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.
 
 ### Database recovery
 
 - Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
 - Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
   ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
 - Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
   migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.
 
 ---
 
 ## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
 
 Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
 
 1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
 2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
 3. runs the selected gates;
 4. exports drill evidence;
 5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
 6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
 
 Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
 silently become an always-on production mirror.
 
 ---

 ## 11. Cost planning
 
 | Environment | Planning envelope |
 |---|---:|
 | Local/integration smoke | `$0–15/month`, depending on whether a shared public callback target is kept |
 | Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |
 | Production Day 1 | **`$120–135/month`** |
 | Production + expected staging | **`$128–153/month`** |
 
 The production baseline includes two `web`, two `discovery`, one worker, DO PostgreSQL primary+standby,
 Cloudflare Free, Sentry Developer, KMS, low-volume logs/offsite backup and egress allowances. Physical read-only
 node, second worker, WAF Pro, Sentry Team and resizing are telemetry-triggered upgrades, not Day-1 requirements.
 
 Host count does not select a resource tier. Scale purchases require staging/production measurements and the
 triggers in DD-04 §5.2.

---
name: gitnexus-pr-review

# PR Review with GitNexus

## When to Use

- "Review this PR"
- "What does PR #42 change?"
- "Is this safe to merge?"
- "What's the blast radius of this PR?"
- "Are there missing tests for this PR?"
- Reviewing someone else's code changes before merge

## Workflow

```
1. gh pr diff <number>                                    → Get the raw diff
2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})  → Map diff to affected flows
3. For each changed symbol:
   gitnexus_impact({target: "<symbol>", direction: "upstream"})    → Blast radius per change
4. gitnexus_context({name: "<key symbol>"})               → Understand callers/callees
5. READ gitnexus://repo/{name}/processes                   → Check affected execution flows
6. Summarize findings with risk assessment
```

> If "Index is stale" → run `npx gitnexus analyze` in terminal before reviewing.

## Checklist

```
- [ ] Fetch PR diff (gh pr diff or git diff base...head)
- [ ] gitnexus_detect_changes to map changes to affected execution flows
- [ ] gitnexus_impact on each non-trivial changed symbol
- [ ] Review d=1 items (WILL BREAK) — are callers updated?
- [ ] gitnexus_context on key changed symbols to understand full picture
- [ ] Check if affected processes have test coverage
- [ ] Assess overall risk level
- [ ] Write review summary with findings
```

## Review Dimensions

| Dimension | How GitNexus Helps |
| --- | --- |
| **Correctness** | `context` shows callers — are they all compatible with the change? |
| **Blast radius** | `impact` shows d=1/d=2/d=3 dependents — anything missed? |
| **Completeness** | `detect_changes` shows all affected flows — are they all handled? |
| **Test coverage** | `impact({includeTests: true})` shows which tests touch changed code |
| **Breaking changes** | d=1 upstream items that aren't updated in the PR = potential breakage |

## Risk Assessment

| Signal | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Changes touch <3 symbols, 0-1 processes | LOW |
| Changes touch 3-10 symbols, 2-5 processes | MEDIUM |
| Changes touch >10 symbols or many processes | HIGH |
| Changes touch auth, payments, or data integrity code | CRITICAL |
| d=1 callers exist outside the PR diff | Potential breakage — flag it |

## Tools

**gitnexus_detect_changes** — map PR diff to affected execution flows:

```
gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})

→ Changed: 8 symbols in 4 files
→ Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow, WebhookHandler
→ Risk: MEDIUM
```

**gitnexus_impact** — blast radius per changed symbol:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})

→ d=1 (WILL BREAK):
  - processCheckout (src/checkout.ts:42) [CALLS, 100%]
  - webhookHandler (src/webhooks.ts:15) [CALLS, 100%]

→ d=2 (LIKELY AFFECTED):
  - checkoutRouter (src/routes/checkout.ts:22) [CALLS, 95%]
```

**gitnexus_impact with tests** — check test coverage:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream", includeTests: true})

→ Tests that cover this symbol:
  - validatePayment.test.ts [direct]
  - checkout.integration.test.ts [via processCheckout]
```

**gitnexus_context** — understand a changed symbol's role:

```
gitnexus_context({name: "validatePayment"})

→ Incoming calls: processCheckout, webhookHandler
→ Outgoing calls: verifyCard, fetchRates
→ Processes: CheckoutFlow (step 3/7), RefundFlow (step 1/5)
```

## Example: "Review PR #42"

```
1. gh pr diff 42 > /tmp/pr42.diff
   → 4 files changed: payments.ts, checkout.ts, types.ts, utils.ts

2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})
   → Changed symbols: validatePayment, PaymentInput, formatAmount
   → Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow
   → Risk: MEDIUM

3. gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: processCheckout, webhookHandler (WILL BREAK)
   → webhookHandler is NOT in the PR diff — potential breakage!

4. gitnexus_impact({target: "PaymentInput", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: validatePayment (in PR), createPayment (NOT in PR)
   → createPayment uses the old PaymentInput shape — breaking change!

5. gitnexus_context({name: "formatAmount"})
   → Called by 12 functions — but change is backwards-compatible (added optional param)

6. Review summary:
   - MEDIUM risk — 3 changed symbols affect 2 execution flows
   - BUG: webhookHandler calls validatePayment but isn't updated for new signature
   - BUG: createPayment depends on PaymentInput type which changed
   - OK: formatAmount change is backwards-compatible
   - Tests: checkout.test.ts covers processCheckout path, but no webhook test
```

## Review Output Format

Structure your review as:

```markdown
## PR Review: <title>

**Risk: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL**

### Changes Summary
- <N> symbols changed across <M> files
- <P> execution flows affected

### Findings
1. **[severity]** Description of finding
   - Evidence from GitNexus tools
   - Affected callers/flows

### Missing Coverage
- Callers not updated in PR: ...
- Untested flows: ...

### Recommendation
APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES / NEEDS DISCUSSION
```

---
name: gitnexus-pr-review
description: "Use when the user wants to review a pull request, understand what a PR changes, assess risk of merging, or check for missing test coverage. Examples: \"Review this PR\", \"What does PR #42 change?\", \"Is this PR safe to merge?\""
---

# PR Review with GitNexus

## When to Use

- "Review this PR"
- "What does PR #42 change?"
- "Is this safe to merge?"
- "What's the blast radius of this PR?"
- "Are there missing tests for this PR?"
- Reviewing someone else's code changes before merge

## Workflow

```
1. gh pr diff <number>                                    → Get the raw diff
2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})  → Map diff to affected flows
3. For each changed symbol:
   gitnexus_impact({target: "<symbol>", direction: "upstream"})    → Blast radius per change
4. gitnexus_context({name: "<key symbol>"})               → Understand callers/callees
5. READ gitnexus://repo/{name}/processes                   → Check affected execution flows
6. Summarize findings with risk assessment
```

> If "Index is stale" → run `npx gitnexus analyze` in terminal before reviewing.

## Checklist

```
- [ ] Fetch PR diff (gh pr diff or git diff base...head)
- [ ] gitnexus_detect_changes to map changes to affected execution flows
- [ ] gitnexus_impact on each non-trivial changed symbol
- [ ] Review d=1 items (WILL BREAK) — are callers updated?
- [ ] gitnexus_context on key changed symbols to understand full picture
- [ ] Check if affected processes have test coverage
- [ ] Assess overall risk level
- [ ] Write review summary with findings
```

## Review Dimensions

| Dimension | How GitNexus Helps |
| --- | --- |
| **Correctness** | `context` shows callers — are they all compatible with the change? |
| **Blast radius** | `impact` shows d=1/d=2/d=3 dependents — anything missed? |
| **Completeness** | `detect_changes` shows all affected flows — are they all handled? |
| **Test coverage** | `impact({includeTests: true})` shows which tests touch changed code |
| **Breaking changes** | d=1 upstream items that aren't updated in the PR = potential breakage |

## Risk Assessment

| Signal | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Changes touch <3 symbols, 0-1 processes | LOW |
| Changes touch 3-10 symbols, 2-5 processes | MEDIUM |
| Changes touch >10 symbols or many processes | HIGH |
| Changes touch auth, payments, or data integrity code | CRITICAL |
| d=1 callers exist outside the PR diff | Potential breakage — flag it |

## Tools

**gitnexus_detect_changes** — map PR diff to affected execution flows:

```
gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})

→ Changed: 8 symbols in 4 files
→ Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow, WebhookHandler
→ Risk: MEDIUM
```

**gitnexus_impact** — blast radius per changed symbol:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})

→ d=1 (WILL BREAK):
  - processCheckout (src/checkout.ts:42) [CALLS, 100%]
  - webhookHandler (src/webhooks.ts:15) [CALLS, 100%]

→ d=2 (LIKELY AFFECTED):
  - checkoutRouter (src/routes/checkout.ts:22) [CALLS, 95%]
```

**gitnexus_impact with tests** — check test coverage:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream", includeTests: true})

→ Tests that cover this symbol:
  - validatePayment.test.ts [direct]
  - checkout.integration.test.ts [via processCheckout]
```

**gitnexus_context** — understand a changed symbol's role:

```
gitnexus_context({name: "validatePayment"})

→ Incoming calls: processCheckout, webhookHandler
→ Outgoing calls: verifyCard, fetchRates
→ Processes: CheckoutFlow (step 3/7), RefundFlow (step 1/5)
```

## Example: "Review PR #42"

```
1. gh pr diff 42 > /tmp/pr42.diff
   → 4 files changed: payments.ts, checkout.ts, types.ts, utils.ts

2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})
   → Changed symbols: validatePayment, PaymentInput, formatAmount
   → Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow
   → Risk: MEDIUM

3. gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: processCheckout, webhookHandler (WILL BREAK)
   → webhookHandler is NOT in the PR diff — potential breakage!

4. gitnexus_impact({target: "PaymentInput", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: validatePayment (in PR), createPayment (NOT in PR)
   → createPayment uses the old PaymentInput shape — breaking change!

5. gitnexus_context({name: "formatAmount"})
   → Called by 12 functions — but change is backwards-compatible (added optional param)

6. Review summary:
   - MEDIUM risk — 3 changed symbols affect 2 execution flows
   - BUG: webhookHandler calls validatePayment but isn't updated for new signature
   - BUG: createPayment depends on PaymentInput type which changed
   - OK: formatAmount change is backwards-compatible
   - Tests: checkout.test.ts covers processCheckout path, but no webhook test
```

## Review Output Format

Structure your review as:

```markdown
## PR Review: <title>

**Risk: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL**

### Changes Summary
- <N> symbols changed across <M> files
- <P> execution flows affected

### Findings
1. **[severity]** Description of finding
   - Evidence from GitNexus tools
   - Affected callers/flows

### Missing Coverage
- Callers not updated in PR: ...
- Untested flows: ...

### Recommendation
APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES / NEEDS DISCUSSION
```

 > **Version:** v2.1 · 2026-07-16
 > **Authority:** DD-04 = DigitalOcean co-location: App Platform + Managed PostgreSQL HA in Singapore,
 > connected through the `sgp1` VPC. The accepted record is
 > [`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`](../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md).
 > **Current repository state:** the local Docker Compose definition exists and validates; Docker-daemon
 > availability remains machine state and **must be checked before each run**. The daemon was started for the
 > 2026-07-16 closure and the canonical PostgreSQL/RLS/auth suite passed; the test container was then stopped.
 > The production DigitalOcean App Spec and deployment workflows are still an infra-slice deliverable.
 > `infra/fly.toml` is a bounded fallback artifact, not the production runbook.
 
 Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
 and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.
 
 ---
 
 ## 0. Target environment model
 
 ```text
 local development
   docker compose + local PostgreSQL
             │
             │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
             ▼
 ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
   production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
   sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
             │
             │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
             ▼
 production on DigitalOcean
   2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
 ```
 
 | Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
 | **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
 | **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
 | **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
 
 Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
 production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
 automatically after the TTL.
 
 ---
 
 ## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
 
 ### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
 
-DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. Record
-written evidence for every production dependency before creating the App Spec:
+DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
+table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
+and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
+`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
+contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
 
-| Dependency | Required answer |
-|---|---|
-| SePay | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Inbound webhook verification alone does not imply that it does. |
-| ApiPay | Does enterprise provisioning restrict API callers by fixed source IP? |
-| Zalo Bot | Does the send API require fixed outbound IP? |
-| Meta Messenger | Does the Graph API require fixed outbound IP? |

# Autopilot slice — DD-04 fixed-egress compatibility evidence

> **Slug:** `infra-egress-compatibility-evidence`  
> **Risk:** P2 docs-only · `manual_only` · STOP_AT_READY · 1 FULL-base Codex clean round  
> **Canonical framework:** `autopilot-prompt-GENERIC` v0.6.0  
> **Authority:** `dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` §7.1 + `standards/environments-setup.md` §1.1  
> **Manifest:** `docs/autopilot-manifests/infra-egress-compatibility-evidence.json`

## Task

```text
Close DD-04 §7.1 with attributable, current evidence for whether Plexco's outbound calls to SePay, ApiPay,
Zalo Bot and Meta Messenger require a fixed public source IP.

Edit only docs/standards/environments-setup.md. Produce a decision-useful evidence table and one overall result.
Do not author deployment configuration, change code/schema/migrations, handle secret values, provision resources,
or infer a provider contract from unrelated inbound webhook requirements.
```

## Evidence contract

Replace the current two-column question table in §1.1 with rows containing:

1. dependency and exact Plexco outbound call surface;
2. fixed source-IP requirement: `NO`, `YES`, or `UNRESOLVED`;
3. primary source title/URL or attributable provider support/contract reference;
4. checked date;
5. concise paraphrase explaining why the source supports the conclusion.

Rules:

- Verify SePay, ApiPay, Zalo Bot and Meta Messenger independently.
- Prefer current official provider documentation. Search snippets, resellers, community posts and model memory
  do not close a row.
- Official documentation silence is not `NO`. If docs are silent, only an attributable provider support or
  contractual response supplied by the owner can close the row.
- Distinguish outbound API source-IP allowlisting from inbound webhook signature, callback IP, domain or app
  review rules. Evidence for one direction cannot answer the other.
- Paraphrase; do not paste long source passages. Record access dates and direct links where available.
- Do not invent prices, enterprise capabilities, allowlists or provider behavior.

## Deterministic outcome

Use this decision table with no silent fall-through:

| Evidence state | Required document result |
|---|---|
| All four rows are attributable and all are `NO` | `Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` |
| All four rows are attributable and one or more are `YES` | `Overall result: FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED` |
| Any row is missing, ambiguous, stale, or only inferred | `HALT-EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE`; no overall result/marker |

Only for either complete result:

- add the literal HTML comment `<!-- DD04_EGRESS_EVIDENCE_CLOSED -->` immediately below the table;
- mark the matching fixed-egress acceptance checklist item complete;
- explain that `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` permits later manual design review only, while
  `FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED` blocks the DO App Platform + VPC green path and requires a separate fallback design.

The marker proves evidence completeness, not that the answer was favorable and not that AP-21 is authorized.

## Negative scope

- No changes outside `docs/standards/environments-setup.md`.
- No App Specs, GitHub Actions, Docker/Fly config, application code, schema, migration or test edits.
- No secret values, credentials, login/session changes, DNS/webhook mutations, provider provisioning or spend.
- No fallback implementation and no weakening of the accepted private DB/VPC decision.
- Do not edit the canonical generic prompt, invariant catalog, backlog, manifest or launcher from the worktree.

## Verification

```bash
pnpm exec prettier --check docs/standards/environments-setup.md
git diff --check
git diff --stat main...HEAD
```

The final diff must be non-empty and contain exactly `docs/standards/environments-setup.md`. Confirm all four
rows have attributable evidence, a checked date and exactly one supported outcome before trusting the marker.
No DB/application suite is required for this docs-only scope; any scope expansion invalidates that exemption.

## Review and stop condition

Register the manifest before edits and create both required counter files. Persist the pinned Codex round with
direct stdout/stderr redirection, never `tee` or a substitute reviewer. P2 docs-only requires one FULL-base
round with zero findings and a real tail verdict on current HEAD. Quota/auth failure halts; no degraded mode.

After verification, run scope-gate `ready`, leave the branch/worktree intact, and STOP_AT_READY. Do not merge,
run AP-21, provision anything, or begin a second task.

## 6. Why option (c) maximizes project benefit

1. **Security:** the steady-state DB path is private; public exposure is not accepted silently to save cost.
2. **Money correctness:** managed standby failover, 99.95% DB SLA, PITR, isolated connection pools, and
   one-provider incident handling reduce failure modes around writes and reconnects.
3. **Maintainability:** one account, region, VPC, bill, and managed compute layer are a better solo-team
   trade than maintaining VMs or AWS network fabric for a small saving.
4. **Scalability:** App Platform can scale components horizontally, and DO Managed PostgreSQL can resize
   CPU/RAM/storage without downtime; expensive capacity is added from telemetry.
5. **Architecture fit:** Plexco needs durable pg-boss and `LISTEN/NOTIFY` session connections, so there is
   no dependency on a serverless DB scale-to-zero lifecycle.
6. **Portability:** immutable images, standard PostgreSQL, configuration-only connection endpoints, IaC,
   and an executable migration contract prevent provider choice from leaking into business logic.

## 7. Risks and acceptance gates

### 7.1 Hard gate: VPC versus dedicated egress IP

DigitalOcean App Platform cannot enable VPC network access and dedicated egress IPs at the same time.
Before implementation, verify whether Zalo, Messenger, SePay, ApiPay, or any other production dependency
requires a fixed outbound source IP.

- If none requires it, proceed with option (c).
- If one requires it, **do not silently disable the private DB path**. Use option `(a) Fly + DO` as the
  bounded fallback, or design a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with both private DB access and controlled egress.

| Dependency | Question to answer | Verification source |
|---|---|---|
| **SePay** | Does Plexco→SePay require source-IP allowlisting? Does inbound webhook verification require a fixed Plexco IP? | SePay partner docs/support; connector spike CO-OQ-2/3/7 |
| **ApiPay** | Does enterprise auto-provisioning (DD-30) restrict API calls by source IP? | ApiPay enterprise onboarding |
| **Zalo Bot** | Does the send API require fixed egress IP? | Zalo Bot API docs; DD-14/15 spike |
| **Messenger (Meta)** | Does the Graph API require fixed egress IP? | Meta docs |
| **Conclusion** | Any confirmed “yes” breaks option (c) as-is and activates the bounded fallback/design branch | Recorded provider evidence |

### 7.2 Provider-neutral application contract

Co-location intentionally accepts DigitalOcean account/region concentration instead of buying an AWS
control plane on Day 1. That risk is acceptable only while the off-provider backup, restore rehearsal,
immutable image, and migration contract below remain green.

The application must satisfy all of these before production:

- build one immutable OCI image that runs on App Platform, Fly, Droplets, DOKS, ECS, or equivalent;
- read provider endpoints only from `DATABASE_URL`, `DATABASE_URL_DIRECT`, and
  `DATABASE_URL_LISTEN`; no provider hostname or SDK in domain/business code;
- use standard PostgreSQL plus an extension allowlist verified against at least two credible target
  providers; migrations own schema, roles, grants, RLS, extensions, and sequence setup;
- keep queue durability in PostgreSQL/pg-boss and realtime durability in canonical tables/outbox;
  `LISTEN/NOTIFY` is a wake-up hint, not the durable record;
- keep provider-specific deployment modules behind IaC/config boundaries;
- do not add a permanent cross-cloud DB proxy only for portability: it adds latency and a new single point
  of failure without removing migration work.

### 7.3 Upgrade paths that preserve application performance

- **App Platform:** scale `web` and `discovery` horizontally. Request-rate and p95-response-time
  autoscaling can run on shared or dedicated compute; CPU-utilization autoscaling requires dedicated CPU.
  Start shared/scalable and move to dedicated only when CPU isolation or CPU-based policy is justified.
- **PostgreSQL:** resize CPU/RAM/storage and enable storage autoscaling before introducing a new data layer.
  DO documents these resize operations as no-downtime, but Plexco still validates reconnects and p95/p99.
- **Compute substrate:** if App Platform cost or network features become limiting, blue-green the same OCI
  image to DO Droplets or DOKS in the same VPC, then drain old instances. The DB path and app contracts stay
  unchanged.
- **Database provider:** provision the target at equal-or-greater tested capacity and the same or newer
  PostgreSQL major version. Migrate with logical replication and a bounded write freeze; do not restore a
  stale dump over a live target.

  outside the DigitalOcean account/provider.
- Export schema, migrations, roles/grants, RLS, extensions, sequence state, and data; a data-only dump is
  not a portable recovery set.
- Run logical exports off-peak with bandwidth/concurrency caps and abort if production p95/p99 or queue
  SLO regresses. At larger data sizes, use a temporary replica/target for the initial copy instead of paying
  for a permanent monthly migration replica.
- Quarterly, restore to temporary vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema checksum,
  row-count, ledger/allocation/outbox reconciliation, smoke tests, and representative load tests.
- Day-1 temporary portability/restore target allowance is approximately **`$10–30 per drill`** plus egress;
  this is a drill cost, not an always-on monthly standby.

### 7.6 Production acceptance checklist

- [ ] §7.1 fixed-egress-IP gate is closed with written provider evidence.
- [ ] App Platform is deployed in `sgp` and connected to the correct `sgp1` VPC.
- [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only the required application private identity.
- [ ] All four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener connection strings work on the private route with TLS
      CA + hostname verification enabled.
- [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` topology passes health-check and rolling-deploy tests.
- [ ] Discovery pool caps, timeouts, rate limits, cache policy, and circuit breaker protect money pools.
- [ ] Primary-failure drill proves automatic DB failover and application reconnection.
- [ ] SSE listeners re-subscribe; pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
- [ ] PITR and off-provider restore drills prove the selected RPO/RTO and reconciliation checks.
- [ ] KMS envelope encryption, key rotation, and degraded/fail-closed paths are tested.
- [ ] Ephemeral staging is reproducible from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
- [ ] Load tests record concurrency, RPS, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate,

## DD-03 — Pricing/tier subscription host (4 tier theo kênh) · 🔄 Thay bằng DD-29

> **2026-07-05:** Bị thay bởi **DD-29** (Phase-1 pricing pivot: free web + 3 bot, 1 gói Host Pro 250k). Nội dung dưới giữ làm **lịch sử** — KHÔNG dùng làm pricing hiện hành.

- **Bối cảnh:** Cần pricing rõ cho host subscription theo giá trị kênh sử dụng.
- **Quyết định:** 4 tier theo tháng/CLB:
  - `free_web` — **0đ**: Web app.
  - `telegram` — **79.000đ**: Web app + bot Telegram.
  - `plus_channel` — **159.000đ**: Web app + bot Telegram + **1 kênh chọn thêm**: Zalo hoặc Messenger.
  - `all_channels` — **199.000đ**: Web app + bot Telegram + bot Zalo + bot Messenger.
- **Lý do:** Giữ cửa vào miễn phí cho web app, monetization tăng theo số kênh chat mà host cần vận hành.
- **Hệ quả:** Billing phải version plan/tier; entitlement channel gate theo plan. Messenger vẫn theo P4.8/post-MVP, quyền Messenger chỉ unlock khi kênh GA.
- **Còn lại:** Chốt lifecycle timing riêng (trial/past_due/suspended ~ 14/7 ngày — TDD Q4) và chính sách cancellation/refund/credit.

Mitigation bắt buộc: **offsite encrypted backup + restore-to-another-Postgres runbook** (chống concentration risk). Cost planning (record Rev 2 — Day-1 baseline + trigger-based): **`$120–135/mo` Day-1 baseline** (2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker × 1 GiB + DO PG HA `$60.90` + Cloudflare Free + Sentry Developer + KMS `$1–3` + logs/R2 `$0–5` + egress `$0–8`) — **money-safe nhờ non-negotiables + read-isolation controls** (projection-role, bounded pool, reserved budget, CDN, circuit-breaker), KHÔNG cần read-replica/WAF-Pro/Sentry-Team Day-1. - **Bối cảnh:** Cần chọn nhà cung cấp Postgres cho hệ tiền multi-tenant RLS; compute app ban đầu target Fly.io — **DD-04 chốt (c) → DO App Platform** (DD-10 giữ nguyên: standalone container).

- **Gate cứng (đã đạt qua (c)):** **auto-failover HA + uptime SLA + posture private-network** (VPC by construction).
- **QĐ-1 — posture network:** (a) public TLS + `sslmode=verify-full` + static-egress-IP allowlist [giữ Fly — **fallback**] · (b) PrivateLink AWS [rời Fly — loại] · **(c) co-locate compute+DB cùng cloud [đổi target Fly→DO, KHÔNG đảo DD-10] — ✅ ĐÃ CHỌN**.
- **QĐ-2 — ứng viên (theo posture a):**
  - **DigitalOcean Managed [nghiêng chọn]** — auto-failover standby + SLA 99.95% + PITR; bill phẳng, vận hành đơn giản; margin dự đoán chắc nhất (không nhất thiết rẻ nhất).
  - **Neon Scale** — HA multi-AZ **mọi plan**; SLA + PrivateLink ở Scale (PrivateLink **AWS-only → vô dụng từ Fly**); consumption floor 0.25 CU, trần = max-CU; rẻ hơn ở tải thấp nhưng cần benchmark.
  - **Supabase** — Pro và Team **rớt uptime-SLA gate**. Team từ `$599` có AWS PrivateLink nhưng không uptime SLA; SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom, còn auto-failover phải contract-confirm. Không cost-comparable với finalist Day-1.
  - **Self-host / Railway-HA** — biến thể self-host (Patroni…), **không** Day-1 (hệ tiền + solo dev): tiết kiệm hạ tầng bị xoá bởi chi phí lao động/rủi ro.
- **Sửa dữ kiện so với lần thu hẹp trước:** (1) Neon HA **không** Scale-gated (multi-AZ mọi plan; Scale = SLA/PrivateLink/IP-allow); (2) Supabase Pro/Team không có uptime SLA; Team từ `$599` chỉ thêm PrivateLink, SLA ở Enterprise custom và auto-failover cần contract confirmation; (3) private-networking **chưa** được giải quyết bởi "Fly private networking" (khác cloud).
- **Tiêu chí:** auto-failover, SLA, posture network, RLS (Postgres core ở mọi provider), pooler/direct-conn cho pg-boss/`LISTEN`, chi phí *dự đoán được*, độ trễ Singapore, maintainability team nhỏ, **portability (chỉ Postgres thuần)**.
- **Hệ quả:** RPO/RTO chốt sau provision + restore drill; benchmark tải trước khi cam kết chi phí (đặc biệt Neon consumption). Không đổi domain model.
- **✅ Đã chốt (2026-07-16):** QĐ-1 = **(c) co-locate DO**; QĐ-2 = **DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG). **Còn lại TRƯỚC khi build infra slice:** (1) **§7.1 egress-IP gate** (verify SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger fixed-outbound-IP); (2) production acceptance **§7.6** (auto-failover drill · PITR restore drill · 4 DB roles · SSE reconnect · pg-boss no-dup · budget alerts); (3) benchmark cost ở staging trước procurement.
- **Chi tiết:** **`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` (final ✅)** · `../dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md` (rev 5, phân tích/nguồn) · `deployment-infra.md §2/§6/§9 (DD-04)`.

## DD-05 — Join cross-club: open vs approval · ✅ Chốt (2026-07-13) — `../dd-05-join-policy-analysis.md`
> **Chốt:** per-session, **mặc định `open`**. **`pending` join_request KHÔNG tạo membership/player/charge**. Final open/approve lock session + eligibility re-check (DD-41) dưới tenant RLS: còn slot → membership+active player+charge+venue; full → membership+waitlisted player, không charge/venue (DD-45). Approve là tenant state-write; capacity-available branch là money-write.

- **Bối cảnh:** Player join kèo từ marketplace xuyên CLB.
- **Quyết định (đề xuất):** Cấu hình **per-kèo**, mặc định **open**, host có thể bật **approval**.
- **Lý do:** Cân bằng tốc độ lấp slot (open) và quyền kiểm soát của host.
- **Hệ quả:** Thêm field cấu hình ở session; state machine join có nhánh chờ duyệt.

## DD-06 — Độ chi tiết vị trí public · ✅ Chốt (2026-07-13) — `../dd-06-location-granularity-analysis.md`
> **Chốt:** public projector emit **`area` (quận/khu vực) only**; `venue_name`/`venue_address`+SĐT/bản đồ **chỉ lộ sau join/approval**. Invariant test: venue KHÔNG vào `public_sessions`.

docs/standards/decisions.md-96-> Contract đầy đủ + nguồn ở `../dd-02-paycode-analysis.md` (rev 6). Tóm tắt canonical dưới đây. **Phase = P4** (khớp BL-PAY-3; sửa drift "P3" cũ).

docs/standards/decisions.md-99-- **Quyết định (owner):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-100-  - **DB lưu suffix trần UPPERCASE, 4 ký tự**, alphabet **A-Z + 2-9 trừ `0 O 1 I L`** (31, không phải Crockford); unique per-tenant; **immutable**; `CHECK (format + ≥1 chữ cái, loại toàn số)`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-101-  - **Prefix = bảng global `pay_prefix_registry(prefix PK, tenant_id, state current|retired, locked_at)` — nguồn DUY NHẤT, KHÔNG cột `tenants.pay_prefix`.** `UNIQUE one-current-per-tenant`; retired KHÔNG tái dụng (PK cho 1 tx thắng). **MVP: prefix IMMUTABLE vĩnh viễn sau code đầu tiên** (bỏ controlled-change — `memberships` chỉ lưu suffix nên đổi prefix sẽ resolve nhầm; tương lai cần thì phải có mapping `(membership_id,prefix,suffix,issued_at)`). **Choke-point atomic** phát-code ↔ đổi-prefix (`SELECT current-prefix FOR UPDATE` cùng lock order). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-102-  - **Recognition = resolver `own | foreign | none`** (narrow SECURITY DEFINER, fail-closed — role `app` không blanket cross-tenant, `0009`). **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`, không false-issued). Prefix BẮT BUỘC; regex thêm `-?`. **Memo-level precedence:** hard-token (foreign / own-prefix+unissued / dash-lạ / error / >1-candidate) → HARD unmatched (thắng name-fallback); ≥2 distinct own → unmatched; đúng 1 own → resolve; chỉ `none`(`PAYMENT`) → name-fallback.
docs/standards/decisions.md-103-  - **Allocation = INVARIANT** qua **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (pay_code chỉ resolve MEMBERSHIP; ledger quyết charge): auto `exact`/`over`/`under`(FIFO partial, edge #8) **+ unknown-fee-phía-sau (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; manual **unknown-fee-đầu**/`already-paid`; ≥2 code / provider-code-mismatch → unmatched.
docs/standards/decisions.md:104:  - **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở core. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30) — parse `data.content` (schema reverify khi build); **SePay = fallback**, `code` chỉ evidence; giữ webhook nhận tx không-code (không `skip_if_no_code`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-105-  - **Merge:** pay_code giữ trên tombstone; recognition **follow `merged_into_membership_id`** (tenant/status/cycle guards). `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện.
docs/standards/decisions.md-106-- **Propagation ĐÃ làm ở SPEC (2026-07-13):** `identity-model` §merge + `feature_identity_tech` (pay_code tombstone follow-through) ✅ · connector `feature_connector_onboarding.md` 4.3b + `_tech` M2 (ApiPay enterprise = Phase-1 **primary**) + **schema `provider_connections.auth_mode += 'enterprise'`** (CHECK shape đầy đủ per provider/auth_mode; platform credential từ KMS, không per-tenant) + **CO-OQ-6 đóng** (`skip_if_no_code=0`, adapter-only) ✅ · `feature_payment_reconcile_tech` **§4 worker pipeline** (`normalize→discover→resolve→precedence→providerCode-crosscheck→plan`) + `bank_transactions.provider_code_enc` + §8 fixtures ✅.
docs/standards/decisions.md-107-- **Propagation CÒN LẠI (khi build P4, code):** wire core + **thay tests** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`); implement **`discoverPayCodeCandidates` + resolver narrow fn + `planIncomingAllocations()`**; migration **`pay_prefix_registry`** (one-current-per-tenant, choke-point) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code`.

docs/standards/decisions.md:359:## DD-30 — Provider onboarding: APIpay enterprise auto-provision per-host · ✅ Chốt
docs/standards/decisions.md-360-
docs/standards/decisions.md:361:- **Bối cảnh:** Onboarding hiện tại (`feature_connector_onboarding`, Draft) = mỗi host **BYO** (SePay OAuth / ApiPay api_key). Để **launch nhanh**, dùng **quan hệ enterprise với APIpay** ở cấp platform để **auto-provision** webhook/bank-account cho từng host (khỏi bắt host tự OAuth/nhập key).
docs/standards/decisions.md-362-- **Quyết định (chốt):** Platform giữ **APIpay enterprise credential**; onboarding host = platform gọi APIpay enterprise API đăng ký **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/:token`. **Tiền vẫn về tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host** — **money-custody KHÔNG đổi**, giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn theo `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**).
docs/standards/decisions.md-363-- **Lý do:** Giảm ma sát onboarding (self-serve chậm/khó với host phổ thông); tận dụng enterprise để auto-register. Vì không đảo money-custody nên **không** đụng hard rule / rủi ro pháp lý trung gian thanh toán.
docs/standards/decisions.md-364-- **Hệ quả:**
docs/standards/decisions.md:365:  - `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (Draft — sửa được): thêm path **"APIpay enterprise auto-provision"** (platform credential, per-host account/webhook), giữ BYO như fallback; provider vẫn `apipay`; verify HMAC `ApiPay-Signature` per-host secret không đổi.
docs/standards/decisions.md-366-  - **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential platform = tài sản KMS; lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → cần rotation + audit riêng. Cần điều khoản enterprise (giới hạn số account/webhook, phí) từ APIpay.

docs/standards/decisions.md-370-

docs/standards/decisions.md-372-
docs/standards/decisions.md-373-- **Bối cảnh:** `feature_web_auth_onboarding(.md/_tech)` đã Reviewed, nhưng lần triển khai đầu (`feat/auth-onboarding-v2`) trải qua 9 round codex review liên tiếp kể từ lúc resume (06→07→08→09), mỗi round phát hiện **một finding khác nhau ở một file khác nhau** — không phải region-thrash (không lặp cùng 1 vùng) mà là pattern "distinct findings spreading across nhiều file round sau round", điều mà standing invariants coi là dấu hiệu slice quá lớn/under-specified chứ không phải thrash đơn thuần. `HALT-MAX_ROUNDS.txt` (round-06, P2 logout-CSRF, đã fix khi resume) và `AWAIT-FOUNDER-round09-nonconvergence.txt` (round-09, P2 unkeyed lookup hash, chưa fix) đều dừng lại chờ founder quyết định thay vì tự vá tiếp lần thứ N. Trước khi build v3, cần một **invariant + test matrix thực thi được** (`BE/auth-security-invariant-table.md`) để design pass không lặp lại kiểu non-convergence này.
docs/standards/decisions.md-374-- **Quyết định (đề xuất):** Chốt danh mục 8 bất biến bảo mật (SI-1..SI-8, chi tiết đầy đủ + test cụ thể + schema ref ở `BE/auth-security-invariant-table.md`) làm scope bắt buộc cho `auth-onboarding-v3`. Trong đó **4 mục còn open ở mức implementation/spec-pin** — **founder decisions đã resolved 2026-07-06** (OQ-1/2/3, xem §5 invariant table), chỉ còn code + pin spec, **KHÔNG chờ founder nữa**:
docs/standards/decisions.md-375-  1. **SI-1 — Pre-auth login-state CSRF binding:** cơ chế state token single-use/short-TTL/browser-bound (3 artifact mới: migration `auth_login_state`, route issue login-state, callback consume) — bổ sung cho (không thay thế) `sameOriginRequest()` hiện tại.
docs/standards/decisions.md-376-  2. **SI-3 — Explicit tenant-filter policy:** chốt làm **policy bằng văn bản** (mọi query tenant-scoped mới trên bề mặt auth/onboarding phải dùng CẢ `withTenant` LẪN predicate `tenant_id` tường minh) + **PORT code fix round-07** (`onboardingSnapshot`) từ v2 sang v3 (base `feat/phase1-fable5` không có).
docs/standards/decisions.md-377-  3. **KMS/secret source wiring (historical pre-merge gap):** **đã chốt 2026-07-06 (OQ-2): AWS KMS** cho staging/prod, dev local giữ fallback `ENCRYPTION_KEY`; runtime delivery qua encrypted-env boundary, không phụ thuộc Fly. Helper dùng chung đã land trong Auth v3; provider port Google/email phải reuse.
--
docs/standards/decisions.md-537-- **Bối cảnh:** `feature_session_management` FE spec **SES-UC-9** hứa *"leave trước giờ chơi → slot freed, waitlist auto-promote"*, nhưng `players.status` chỉ có `active|left` (`players_status_chk`) — **chưa model** waitlist. Cả 2 app đối thủ ngầm có khái niệm đầy chỗ.
docs/standards/decisions.md-538-- **Quyết định:** Mở `players.status` thêm `waitlisted`, `waitlist_position`, CHECK `waitlisted ⇔ position non-null` + position dương, queue index và total order `position,joined_at,id`. Open join/approval khi full cùng tạo waitlisted row, không charge/venue. Queue entry/deprioritize gán `max(position)+1` dưới session lock; rank không cần contiguous/monotonic lịch sử. Leave/capacity+ re-count active rồi promote theo vòng lặp tới khi đầy; finite→unlimited promote mọi eligible. Promotion chỉ mutate sau khi assert chưa có charge và derive amount thành công; existing charge hoặc DD-39 derivation failure đều giữ candidate waitlisted, audit/owner-review, tiếp tục candidate kế. Derivation failure ghi một canonical outbox event keyed by session+player+trigger+reason; retry và alert là consumers, không tự re-derive; không rollback opener. Capacity decrease không promote/evict và `slots_max < active_count` → `CAPACITY_BELOW_ROSTER`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-539-- **Rejoin (DD-49 supersedes default fail-closed):** Existing `active|waitlisted` trả current outcome idempotently. Existing `left` chạy lại eligibility/capacity/ban; chưa refund thì carry-over obligation/allocation hiệu lực, đã refund thì append obligation lifecycle mới trong cùng canonical aggregate. Live UNIQUE charge/player/session cấm tạo charge đôi. Nếu correction core chưa land hoặc không chứng minh được disposition thì mới trả `REJOIN_REQUIRES_REVIEW`; không reopen/void/reset charge mù.
docs/standards/decisions.md-540-- **Lý do:** Đóng gap giữa spec đã Reviewed và data model thực tế; nhu cầu thật khi kèo full.
docs/standards/decisions.md-541-- **Hệ quả:** Migration status+position CHECK+index; capacity/projector chỉ đếm active; triggering command + canonical failure-event key cung cấp idempotency nên retry không double-promote/charge. Product/BE session + cross-club + DD-39 charge-derivation + notification specs đã pin full→waitlist, rejoin guard và derivation-failure behavior; schema/code migration vẫn pending build.

docs/standards/decisions.md-564-## DD-47 — Social follow graph (person↔person) · ✅ Chốt framework (2026-07-13), build P4+ — `../dd-47-follow-graph-analysis.md`
docs/standards/decisions.md-565-> **Chốt (owner): duyệt framework, build P4+.** Bảng GLOBAL `person_follows` (directed, `pending/accepted`, no `tenant_id`). **⚠️ = GLOBAL PROTECTED** (như persons/accounts): grants theo `0009` — **no blanket grant cho `app`, chỉ qua identity service/`SECURITY DEFINER`** (không chỉ app choke-point). Auto-accept public/approve private (DD-48); **KHÔNG tiền/PII/roster**; merge re-parent+dedupe.
docs/standards/decisions.md-566-
docs/standards/decisions.md-567-- **Bối cảnh:** `feature_user_settings` yêu cầu **cơ chế follow** (theo dõi) giữa các user. Plexco hiện **KHÔNG có social graph**: `persons` là anchor global (no `tenant_id`); `feature_player_view` chỉ cho xem dữ liệu của **chính mình** (person-self) — chưa có "user A theo dõi user B" hay "xem hồ sơ user khác". `memberships`/`players` là quan hệ person↔tenant/session, **KHÔNG** phải person↔person → không có bảng nào diễn tả được quan hệ theo dõi.
docs/standards/decisions.md-568-- **Quyết định:** Thêm bảng **GLOBAL PROTECTED** `person_follows` (cạnh follower→followee, không `tenant_id`).
