docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-192----
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-193-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-194-## 3. API Contract
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-195-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-196-### Endpoints nội bộ (nâng cấp `/api/onboarding/*`)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-197-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-198-| Method | Path | Auth | Mô tả |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-199-|--------|------|------|-------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-200-| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/oauth/start` | TenantOwner (cookie+CSRF) | Tạo `oauth_transactions` (state+PKCE) → trả authorize URL |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-201-| GET  | `/api/onboarding/sepay/oauth/callback` | **state-bound (PKCE), KHÔNG cookie** | `consume_oauth_transaction(state)` SECURITY DEFINER → exchange token → `GET /api/v1/bank-accounts` → rotate session |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-202-| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/webhooks/register` | TenantOwner | `POST /api/v1/webhooks` cho TK đã chọn (contract product §4.2.2) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-203-| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/token/connect` | TenantOwner | Explicit outbound fallback: persist KMS token under `auth_mode='api_token'`, then register webhook; no auth-mode downgrade |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-204-| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/provision` | TenantOwner | **Phase-1 primary:** platform enterprise credential provision/bind bank+webhook riêng của host; client không gửi platform secret |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:205:| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/connect` | TenantOwner | **Explicit BYO fallback:** accessKey+secretKey → `GET /v2/client/banks` (lọc `ACTIVE`) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:206:| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/webhooks/register` | TenantOwner | **BYO fallback:** `POST /v1/client/webhooks` cho TK `ACTIVE` đã chọn |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-207-| POST | `/api/onboarding/zalo/connect` 🟡 | TenantOwner | *(blocked CO-OQ-1)* bot token → `getMe` → `setWebhook` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-208-| GET  | `/api/onboarding/connectors/status` | TenantOwner | Status từng connector theo vocabulary §2 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-209-| DELETE | `/api/onboarding/connectors/:provider` | TenantOwner | Gỡ kết nối (revoke + delete webhook → `revoked`) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-210-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-211-### `POST /api/onboarding/apipay/provision` — enterprise primary contract
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-212-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-213-**Headers:** cookie session + CSRF của TenantOwner; `Idempotency-Key: <UUID>` bắt buộc. Key được persist theo `(tenant_id, route, key)` cùng `request_hash` và response canonical; không chỉ giữ trong memory/cache.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-214-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-215-```jsonc
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-216-// Request
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-217-{
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-218-  "bankSelectionRef": "bsr_opaque_server_issued",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-219-  "consent": true
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-220-}
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-221-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-222-// 201 lần đầu; retry cùng key + cùng body trả 200 với đúng các ID này
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-223-{
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-224-  "provider": "apipay",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-225-  "authMode": "enterprise",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-226-  "connectionId": "uuid",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-227-  "bankAccountId": "uuid",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-228-  "status": "registered",
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-229-  "verifyHint": "Chuyển khoản thử để xác minh"
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-230-}
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-231-```
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-232-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-233-- `bankSelectionRef` là reference opaque, TTL ngắn, do server cấp sau flow host chọn/xác nhận TK theo cơ chế enterprise đã được ApiPay phê duyệt; nó bound với tenant + owner + bank selection. Nó **không** phải accessKey/secretKey, platform credential, số TK thô hay provider webhook secret. Nếu enterprise contract thực tế không cung cấp được reference tương đương, HALT production mapping và xác nhận lại provider request shape; không tự mở input PII/secret mới.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-234-- Request schema `additionalProperties=false`: reject mọi field credential/secret ngoài shape trên. `consent` phải đúng `true`; resolve ref + authorization + TTL trước provider I/O.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-235-- Handler lock logical `(tenant_id, provider='apipay')`, upsert `provider_connections.auth_mode='enterprise'`, rồi provision/bind bank + webhook. Provider-side replay phải dùng **một cơ chế đã được enterprise contract chứng minh**: stable derived idempotency key nếu provider hỗ trợ, hoặc exact lookup/reuse bằng deterministic provision ref. Không có cơ chế nào → trả `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE`, không gọi create mù sau timeout. Enterprise credential chỉ đọc từ platform KMS, không lưu vào tenant row, response, analytics hay log.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-236-- Provider success + DB commit fail đi qua orphan cleanup §4.2; retry cùng key phải reuse provider resource/response, không tạo bank/webhook thứ hai. Cùng key nhưng body hash khác → `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` trước provider call.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:237:- Tenant đã có compatible enterprise binding → trả resource hiện hữu. Muốn đổi bank hoặc đổi `enterprise ↔ api_key` phải qua replace/revoke flow explicit; endpoint không silently downgrade sang BYO.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:238:- Enterprise unavailable/unsupported chỉ trả error có type để UI hiện CTA **"Dùng ApiPay key riêng"**; BYO endpoints không được gọi tự động.
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-239-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-240-### Webhook receiver (provider-initiated)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-241-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-242-| Method | Path | Verify (timing-safe trên RAW body) | Delegate |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-243-|--------|------|-----------------------------------|----------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-244-| POST | `/webhook/sepay/:token` | Default `hmac_sha256`: `X-SePay-Signature=sha256=<hex>` over `{X-SePay-Timestamp}.{rawBody}`, freshness ±300s. Explicit fallback `api_key`: exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` | bank_transactions ingest |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-245-| POST | `/webhook/apipay/:token` | `ApiPay-Signature` == HMAC-SHA256(rawBody, `webhook_secret`) | bank_transactions ingest |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-246-| POST | `/webhook/zalo/:token` 🟡 | Narrow endpoint resolve → require ciphertext + `connecting|registered|active|drift` → KMS-decrypt `zalo_bots.webhook_secret_enc` → timing-safe compare `X-Bot-Api-Secret-Token`; `connecting` without ciphertext plus `error|revoked` fail closed; no plaintext fallback | channel event handler |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-248-### Rate Limits (BL-SEC-5)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-249-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-250-| Endpoint | Limit |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-251-|----------|-------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-252-| `*/connect`, `*/provision`, `*/webhooks/register` (gọi provider API) | 5 / 10 phút / tenant |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-253-| `*/oauth/start`, `*/oauth/callback` | 10 / phút / IP |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-254-| `DELETE /connectors/:provider` | 5 / phút / tenant |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-255-| `POST /webhook/{provider}/:token` | Không áp app-level user limit; edge per-token/IP burst cap + body-size cap theo `deployment-infra.md §6` (sized ≥100/s/token target) |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-261-| 400 | `APIPAY_BANK_SELECTION_INVALID` | "Phiên chọn tài khoản không hợp lệ hoặc đã hết hạn" | ref sai tenant/owner, expired, consent thiếu hoặc request có field ngoài schema |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-262-| 409 | `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` | "Yêu cầu này đã được dùng cho dữ liệu khác" | cùng idempotency key, request hash khác |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:263:| 422 | `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_BANK_UNSUPPORTED` | "Ngân hàng này chưa được hỗ trợ tự động" | enterprise không phủ bank; UI có thể mời BYO |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-264-| 502 | `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_PROVISION_FAILED` | "Chưa thể thiết lập ApiPay, vui lòng thử lại" | provider create/bind fail; retry cùng key |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:265:| 503 | `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE` | "Kết nối tự động tạm chưa sẵn sàng" | platform KMS credential/enterprise release gate unavailable; UI có thể mời BYO |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-266-| 401 | `APIPAY_KEY_INVALID` | "accessKey/secretKey không đúng" | `GET /v2/client/banks` 401 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-267-| 409 | `APIPAY_BANK_NOT_ACTIVE` | "Tài khoản chưa kích hoạt, vui lòng đợi" | bank `PENDING`/`manualActivationPending` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-268-| 502 | `PROVIDER_WEBHOOK_REGISTER_FAILED` | "Đăng ký webhook thất bại" | provider API lỗi → fallback |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-269-| 401 | `SEPAY_OAUTH_DENIED` | "Bạn chưa cấp quyền cho Plexco" | OAuth deny/timeout |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-270-| 400 | `OAUTH_STATE_INVALID` | "Phiên kết nối hết hạn, thử lại" | state không khớp/đã dùng/hết hạn |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-271-| 400 | `ZALO_TOKEN_INVALID` 🟡 | "Token bot không hợp lệ" | `getMe` fail |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-272-| 409 | `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` | "TK ngân hàng đã gắn ở CLB khác" | fingerprint trùng tenant khác |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-273-| 401 | `INVALID_WEBHOOK_SECRET` | — (không trả nội dung) | verify webhook fail |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-274-| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | "Thao tác quá nhanh, thử lại sau" | vượt BL-SEC-5 |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-276-### Idempotency
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-277-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-278-| Endpoint | Idempotent? | Key |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-279-|----------|-------------|-----|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-280-| `*/webhooks/register` | ✅ | `(tenant_id, provider, provider_bank_ref)` — đã có `provider_webhook_id` thì reuse |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-281-| `apipay/provision` | ✅ | required `(tenant_id, route, Idempotency-Key)` + request hash + persisted canonical response; provider replay dùng verified idempotency key hoặc exact deterministic lookup/reuse; thiếu cả hai thì path fail-closed unavailable; same key/different body → 409 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:282:| `apipay/connect` (BYO), `sepay/oauth/start` | ✅ | upsert `(tenant_id, provider)` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-283-| `oauth/callback` | ✅ | `state` single-use (xóa sau exchange) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-284-| `zalo/connect` 🟡 | ✅ | `(tenant_id)` — `zalo_bots` UNIQUE(tenant) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-285-| Webhook ingest | ✅ | `(tenant, provider, external_id)` (delegate payment ingest) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-286-| Flip Tier 2 | ✅ | CAS `UPDATE ... WHERE status IN ('connecting','registered') RETURNING`; emit analytics only when one row transitions. `active` is idempotent; `drift` is processed without clearing drift; `error|revoked` reject |

docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-288----
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-289-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-290-## 4. Chi tiết triển khai
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-291-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-292-### 4.1 State machine (impl — product §3.3)
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-293-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-294-| Từ | Sự kiện | Đến | Side-effect |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-295-|----|---------|-----|-------------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-296-| `connecting` | validate + autoRegister OK | `registered` | CAS only if still `connecting`; lưu `provider_webhook_id`, `last_register_at`, `auto_registered=true`; if early event already set `active`, update metadata without status downgrade |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-297-| `connecting` | verified event arrives before register finalization | `active` | requires ciphertext; CAS once + emit `connector_verified`; later finalization preserves `active` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-298-| `connecting` | validate/register fail | `error` | `last_error_code`; giữ credential cho retry |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-299-| `registered` | event thật đầu tiên | `active` | emit `connector_verified` (1 lần) |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-300-| `active` | drift cron URL lệch | `drift` | thử re-register |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-301-| `drift` | re-register OK | `registered` | `last_setwebhook_at` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-302-| `drift` | re-register fail | `error` | alert owner |
--
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-399-| 12 | Orphan cleanup | commit-2 fail | webhook bị DELETE |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-400-| 13 | OAuth state hợp lệ | state khớp | exchange OK |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-401-| 14 | OAuth state replay/expired | state đã dùng/hết hạn | `OAUTH_STATE_INVALID` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-402-| 15 | PKCE verifier sai | verifier lệch challenge | exchange fail |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-403-| 16 | Token refresh ngưỡng | hết hạn < 5' | refresh gọi |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-404-| 17 | Refresh fail | refresh token revoked | `status=error`, không ảnh hưởng TK khác |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-405-| 18 | Flip Tier 2 idempotent | 2 event đầu | `active` 1 lần, analytics 1 lần |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-406-| 19 | Idempotent register | gọi 2 lần | reuse `provider_webhook_id`, không tạo trùng |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-407-| 20 | `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` | fingerprint trùng tenant khác | 409 |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-408-| 21 | Secret không vào log | trigger error | log chỉ `last_error_code` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-409-| 22 | Revoke connector | DELETE | webhook xóa, `revoked`, lịch sử txn giữ |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-410-| 23 | Drift auto re-register | URL lệch | re-register → `registered` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-411-| 24 | Rate limit connect | spam | `RATE_LIMITED` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-412-| 25 | SePay API-key fallback exact header | `Authorization: Apikey <key>` vs bare/custom scheme | exact passes constant-time; all variants reject |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-413-| 26 | No downgrade | HMAC configured but invalid signature with valid-looking API key | 401; verifier never tries alternate mode |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-414-| 27 | SePay outbound token shape | `provider='sepay', auth_mode='api_token'` | Only `provider_api_token_enc` present; OAuth/ApiPay credential columns NULL; unsupported mixed shapes fail CHECK |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-415-| 28 | ApiPay enterprise request shape | valid ref+consent vs secret/accessKey/extra field | valid accepted; unknown/credential fields rejected before provider call |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-416-| 29 | ApiPay enterprise ref boundary | expired, wrong-tenant, wrong-owner refs | `APIPAY_BANK_SELECTION_INVALID`; no provider I/O |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-417-| 30 | ApiPay enterprise idempotent retry | same key+body twice, including timeout after provider success | same connection/bank/webhook IDs; one provider resource |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-418-| 31 | ApiPay idempotency conflict | same key, different `bankSelectionRef` | `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED`; no second provider call |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:419:| 32 | ApiPay enterprise fallback is explicit | provider unavailable/unsupported | typed error only; BYO handler is not invoked until owner selects fallback |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-420-| 33 | ApiPay platform secret boundary | success/error/log/tenant row snapshot | no platform credential or webhook secret in response/log/tenant credential columns |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-421-| 34 | ApiPay provider replay gate | enterprise contract has neither idempotency key nor exact deterministic lookup | `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE` before create; no blind retry/provision |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-422-| 35 | Zalo legacy secret migration | backfillable and unprovable legacy rows | KMS ciphertext written for valid row; unprovable row becomes `error`; constraint validates; plaintext column/value removed; no dual-read |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-423-| 36 | Zalo receiver state/ciphertext matrix | every status × ciphertext present/absent | only `connecting|registered|active|drift` with ciphertext may verify; all missing-secret and `error|revoked` cases reject |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-424-| 37 | Zalo early-event CAS | event wins between provider registration and DB finalization | exactly one `connecting→active`; finalization writes metadata but preserves `active`; analytics once |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-425-| 38 | Zalo repeated finalization/event | registered/active/drift plus duplicate event | no `active→registered`, no drift clear, no duplicate analytics or provider/domain mutation |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-426-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-427-### Integration Tests
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-428-
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-429-| # | Test | Setup | Expected |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-430-|---|------|-------|----------|
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-431-| 1 | ApiPay enterprise E2E (primary) | selection ref → provision → webhook txn | `auth_mode='enterprise'`; riêng account/webhook host; `registered`→`active`; txn ingest 1 lần |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-432-| 2 | ApiPay enterprise retry after crash | provider success, DB commit-2 fail, retry same key | provider idempotency/orphan reconciliation yields exactly one bound bank+webhook |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:433:| 3 | ApiPay BYO E2E (fallback) | owner explicitly chooses BYO → connect → register → webhook txn | `auth_mode='api_key'`; `registered`→`active`; no enterprise row mutation |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-434-| 4 | SePay OAuth E2E | start→callback→register→giả lập GD | token lưu, webhook tạo, `active` |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-435-| 5 | OAuth không cookie | callback từ domain ngoài (no cookie) | vẫn thành công nhờ state/PKCE |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-436-| 6 | 2 tenant cô lập | 2 connection, 2 token | routing độc lập, RLS không rò |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-437-| 7 | Crash giữa register | kill sau create-webhook, trước commit-2 | cleanup-orphan dọn webhook thừa |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:438:| 8 | ApiPay PENDING→ACTIVE (BYO) | bank kích hoạt sau | recheck job tự register |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md:439:| 9 | Enterprise bank unsupported → BYO handoff | provision trả 422, owner chọn fallback | không auto-downgrade; BYO form/endpoint chỉ mở sau action của owner |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-440-| 10 | Zalo secret migration E2E | legacy plaintext rows → deploy forward migration → receive webhook | valid rows verify from KMS ciphertext; unprovable rows are non-routable `error`; plaintext column absent |
docs/features/BE/feature_connector_onboarding_tech.md-441-| 11 | Zalo register/event race E2E | pause connect after provider success; deliver signed event; resume finalization | final state `active`, metadata committed, one delivery/analytics event, no downgrade |

## DD-30 — Provider onboarding: APIpay enterprise auto-provision per-host · ✅ Chốt

- **Bối cảnh:** Onboarding hiện tại (`feature_connector_onboarding`, Draft) = mỗi host **BYO** (SePay OAuth / ApiPay api_key). Để **launch nhanh**, dùng **quan hệ enterprise với APIpay** ở cấp platform để **auto-provision** webhook/bank-account cho từng host (khỏi bắt host tự OAuth/nhập key).
- **Quyết định (chốt):** Platform giữ **APIpay enterprise credential**; onboarding host = platform gọi APIpay enterprise API đăng ký **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/:token`. **Tiền vẫn về tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host** — **money-custody KHÔNG đổi**, giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn theo `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**).
- **Lý do:** Giảm ma sát onboarding (self-serve chậm/khó với host phổ thông); tận dụng enterprise để auto-register. Vì không đảo money-custody nên **không** đụng hard rule / rủi ro pháp lý trung gian thanh toán.
- **Hệ quả:**
  - `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (Draft — sửa được): thêm path **"APIpay enterprise auto-provision"** (platform credential, per-host account/webhook), giữ BYO như fallback; provider vẫn `apipay`; verify HMAC `ApiPay-Signature` per-host secret không đổi.
  - **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential platform = tài sản KMS; lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → cần rotation + audit riêng. Cần điều khoản enterprise (giới hạn số account/webhook, phí) từ APIpay.
  - **KHÔNG** đụng ledger/reconcile core — chỉ đường onboarding/provisioning.

## DD-31 — Auth security invariant + test matrix (auth-onboarding v3) · ✅ Merged (Telegram-era)

> **2026-07-10:** v3 ĐÃ MERGE vào `main@1eb6f60` (`5b1193b`) — Telegram-era, kèm migrations `0012/0013` + KMS pepper client (`37296ef`) + tests. Nội dung "🟡 đề xuất / chờ merge / build-new" bên dưới là **pre-merge** (giữ lịch sử). Residual thực sự = audit SI trên main + swap provider Google/email (DD-37/DD-13) — theo dõi dưới DD-13.

  NOT VALID;
ALTER TABLE zalo_bots VALIDATE CONSTRAINT zalo_bots_webhook_secret_ready_chk;
ALTER TABLE zalo_bots DROP COLUMN secret;
```

`webhook_token` remains a random opaque lookup value and is not an authentication secret. Runtime resolves the tenant-bound endpoint through a narrow accessor, requires ciphertext and status `connecting|registered|active|drift`, KMS-decrypts `webhook_secret_enc` only for timing-safe header verification, and never returns/logs/caches plaintext. A `connecting` row without ciphertext plus every `error|revoked` row fails closed. After cutover there is no dual-read of legacy `secret`.

`provider_connections`, `bank_accounts` (đã có), `zalo_bots` (đã có) đều tenant-scoped với **ENABLE + FORCE RLS** dùng `app.tenant_id` (BL-TEN-1). `oauth_transactions` là ngoại lệ bootstrap: direct table access bị revoke khỏi runtime role; callback dùng SECURITY DEFINER `consume_oauth_transaction(state)` để lookup-by-state single-use trước khi set `app.tenant_id`, sau đó mọi ghi tenant-scoped quay lại RLS thường.

> **Vocabulary status** (product §3.1): `connecting → registered`(Tier 1) `→ active`(Tier 2); `drift`/`error`/`revoked`. `provider_connections.status` chỉ do connector lifecycle service ghi: direct provider events (connect/retry/revoke/OAuth refresh) set provider-level state; sau mỗi child update, service recompute rollup từ `bank_accounts.webhook_status` theo product §3.1.1. Không update raw table từ webhook/job/UI ngoài service này.

### Indexes

- `provider_connections (status)` và `(token_expires_at) WHERE auth_mode='oauth2'` — cho job refresh token.
- `bank_accounts (provider_connection_id)`; `(webhook_status) WHERE webhook_status IN ('connecting','registered')` — cho recheck pending / drift.
- `oauth_transactions (expires_at)` — sweep hết hạn.
- `zalo_bots (webhook_token)` — lookup webhook (đã cần ở routing).
- Đã có: `bank_accounts.webhook_token UNIQUE`, `bank_transactions (tenant,provider,external_id) UNIQUE`.

### Edge Cases (Backend)

| # | Category | Case | Xử lý |
|---|----------|------|-------|
| 1 | Security | Tenant suy từ nội dung payload | ❌ NEVER — chỉ `:token`→`webhook_token`, rồi verify `account_fingerprint` (BL-PAY-6) |
| 2 | Security | HMAC verify trên JSON re-serialize | Dùng **raw body** (§4.5); SePay signed input còn gồm exact timestamp prefix; test payload unicode/space lạ |
| 3 | Security | Forged/unknown webhook token | 401, verify secret timing-safe, không persist (BL-SEC-2) |
| 4 | Security | `state` giả/replay ở OAuth callback | `OAUTH_STATE_INVALID`; state single-use, TTL 10', thiếu state → từ chối |
| 5 | Security | Secret/token rò ra log | KMS at-rest, redaction; chỉ lưu `last_error_code` (BL-SEC-1) |
| 6 | Concurrency | 2 lần "Kết nối" đồng thời | `UNIQUE(tenant,provider)` + upsert; chỉ 1 webhook đăng ký |
| 7 | Concurrency | Event đến trước khi `registered` commit | Chỉ `connecting` + ciphertext được verify; hợp lệ → CAS `connecting→active`. Register finalization CAS `connecting→registered` và preserve `active`; thiếu ciphertext/`error|revoked` reject |
| 8 | Concurrency | Replay event nhà cung cấp | Flip Tier 2 idempotent; ingest dedup `(tenant,provider,external_id)` |
| 9 | Data Integrity | Provider tạo webhook OK nhưng DB commit fail | Lưu `provider_webhook_id` cùng transaction; lệch → job `connectors/cleanup-orphan-webhook` gọi `DELETE` webhook mồ côi |
| 10 | Data Integrity (ApiPay) | TK `PENDING`/`manualActivationPending` | Không register; `webhook_status='connecting'`; job `connectors/recheck-pending-bank` (≤24h) |
| 11 | Data Integrity | Drift — owner đổi URL ở dashboard | `security/webhook-drift` (hourly) → auto re-register; fail → `drift`/alert |
| 12 | Token lifecycle | SePay refresh token hết hạn/refresh fail | `status='error'` + nhắc kết nối lại; **không** ảnh hưởng connection/TK khác |
| 13 | Token lifecycle | ApiPay BYO key bị thu hồi bên ngoài | Delivery fail liên tục → `error` + alert; runbook rotate (enterprise credential có platform-level rotation/audit riêng) |
| 14 | Cross-Feature | Gỡ connector | revoke OAuth / `DELETE webhook` → `status='revoked'`; **giữ** lịch sử `bank_transactions` |
| 15 | Cross-Feature | `BANK_ALREADY_BOUND` (fingerprint trùng tenant khác) | 409, không bind; tránh route nhầm tenant |
| 16 | Abuse | Spam `*/connect` dò key | Rate limit BL-SEC-5 (§3) |
| 17 | Security | SePay HMAC stale/future timestamp, bad signature hoặc missing header | 401 trước parse/persist; no auth downgrade |
| 18 | Security | SePay API-key fallback header sai | Chỉ exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` constant-time; reject bare/custom header |

---

## 3. API Contract

### Endpoints nội bộ (nâng cấp `/api/onboarding/*`)

| Method | Path | Auth | Mô tả |
|--------|------|------|-------|
| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/oauth/start` | TenantOwner (cookie+CSRF) | Tạo `oauth_transactions` (state+PKCE) → trả authorize URL |
| GET  | `/api/onboarding/sepay/oauth/callback` | **state-bound (PKCE), KHÔNG cookie** | `consume_oauth_transaction(state)` SECURITY DEFINER → exchange token → `GET /api/v1/bank-accounts` → rotate session |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/webhooks/register` | TenantOwner | `POST /api/v1/webhooks` cho TK đã chọn (contract product §4.2.2) |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/sepay/token/connect` | TenantOwner | Explicit outbound fallback: persist KMS token under `auth_mode='api_token'`, then register webhook; no auth-mode downgrade |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/provision` | TenantOwner | **Phase-1 primary:** platform enterprise credential provision/bind bank+webhook riêng của host; client không gửi platform secret |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/connect` | TenantOwner | **Explicit BYO fallback:** accessKey+secretKey → `GET /v2/client/banks` (lọc `ACTIVE`) |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/apipay/webhooks/register` | TenantOwner | **BYO fallback:** `POST /v1/client/webhooks` cho TK `ACTIVE` đã chọn |
| POST | `/api/onboarding/zalo/connect` 🟡 | TenantOwner | *(blocked CO-OQ-1)* bot token → `getMe` → `setWebhook` |
| GET  | `/api/onboarding/connectors/status` | TenantOwner | Status từng connector theo vocabulary §2 |
| DELETE | `/api/onboarding/connectors/:provider` | TenantOwner | Gỡ kết nối (revoke + delete webhook → `revoked`) |

### `POST /api/onboarding/apipay/provision` — enterprise primary contract

**Headers:** cookie session + CSRF của TenantOwner; `Idempotency-Key: <UUID>` bắt buộc. Key được persist theo `(tenant_id, route, key)` cùng `request_hash` và response canonical; không chỉ giữ trong memory/cache.

```jsonc
// Request
{
  "bankSelectionRef": "bsr_opaque_server_issued",
  "consent": true
}

// 201 lần đầu; retry cùng key + cùng body trả 200 với đúng các ID này
{
  "provider": "apipay",
  "authMode": "enterprise",
  "connectionId": "uuid",
  "bankAccountId": "uuid",
  "status": "registered",
  "verifyHint": "Chuyển khoản thử để xác minh"
}
```

- `bankSelectionRef` là reference opaque, TTL ngắn, do server cấp sau flow host chọn/xác nhận TK theo cơ chế enterprise đã được ApiPay phê duyệt; nó bound với tenant + owner + bank selection. Nó **không** phải accessKey/secretKey, platform credential, số TK thô hay provider webhook secret. Nếu enterprise contract thực tế không cung cấp được reference tương đương, HALT production mapping và xác nhận lại provider request shape; không tự mở input PII/secret mới.
- Request schema `additionalProperties=false`: reject mọi field credential/secret ngoài shape trên. `consent` phải đúng `true`; resolve ref + authorization + TTL trước provider I/O.
- Handler lock logical `(tenant_id, provider='apipay')`, upsert `provider_connections.auth_mode='enterprise'`, rồi provision/bind bank + webhook. Provider-side replay phải dùng **một cơ chế đã được enterprise contract chứng minh**: stable derived idempotency key nếu provider hỗ trợ, hoặc exact lookup/reuse bằng deterministic provision ref. Không có cơ chế nào → trả `APIPAY_ENTERPRISE_UNAVAILABLE`, không gọi create mù sau timeout. Enterprise credential chỉ đọc từ platform KMS, không lưu vào tenant row, response, analytics hay log.
- Provider success + DB commit fail đi qua orphan cleanup §4.2; retry cùng key phải reuse provider resource/response, không tạo bank/webhook thứ hai. Cùng key nhưng body hash khác → `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` trước provider call.
- Tenant đã có compatible enterprise binding → trả resource hiện hữu. Muốn đổi bank hoặc đổi `enterprise ↔ api_key` phải qua replace/revoke flow explicit; endpoint không silently downgrade sang BYO.
- Enterprise unavailable/unsupported chỉ trả error có type để UI hiện CTA **"Dùng ApiPay key riêng"**; BYO endpoints không được gọi tự động.

### Webhook receiver (provider-initiated)

| Method | Path | Verify (timing-safe trên RAW body) | Delegate |
|--------|------|-----------------------------------|----------|
| POST | `/webhook/sepay/:token` | Default `hmac_sha256`: `X-SePay-Signature=sha256=<hex>` over `{X-SePay-Timestamp}.{rawBody}`, freshness ±300s. Explicit fallback `api_key`: exact `Authorization: Apikey <key>` | bank_transactions ingest |
| POST | `/webhook/apipay/:token` | `ApiPay-Signature` == HMAC-SHA256(rawBody, `webhook_secret`) | bank_transactions ingest |

    49	## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
    50	
    51	### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
    52	
    53	DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
    54	table below records the current attributable evidence state of this gate, one row per production dependency
    55	and its exact Plexco outbound call surface. Evidence rule (DD-04 §7.1): official documentation silence is not
    56	`NO`; a row that rests only on inference stays `UNRESOLVED` until an attributable provider support or
    57	contractual response supplied by the owner closes it.
    58	
    59	| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
    60	|---|---|---|---|---|
    61	| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **UNRESOLVED** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | Official docs specify token/OAuth2 issuance with no source-IP registration step, and the docs' only IP-allowlist page covers the reverse direction (merchants allowlist SePay's webhook egress IPs). No page affirmatively states the outbound-caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable SePay support/contract answer on source-IP policy for `my.sepay.vn` API callers. |
    62	| **ApiPay** — primary: DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning contract (per-host provisioning; contractual surface, not publicly documented) · fallback: BYO-key calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) | **UNRESOLVED** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Published docs fully specify BYO auth (`Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)`) and contain no IP-allowlist or caller-IP-restriction concept (the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A records), but they are silent on caller source-IP policy — and the DD-30 primary enterprise provisioning surface is contractual, so public docs cannot answer it at all. Closes with the ApiPay enterprise onboarding/contract answer covering both surfaces. |
    63	| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **UNRESOLVED** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The published calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL), with no IP-declaration step and no origin/IP error class — but no page affirmatively states the caller source-IP policy, so documentation silence cannot close this row as `NO`. Closes with an attributable Zalo Bot support answer on source-IP policy for `bot-api.zaloplatforms.com` callers. |
    64	| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta affirmatively documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
    65

    67	overall result is recorded and the closure marker is intentionally absent: their official documentation never
